Method: 提出多阶段LLM流水线，包含确定性预处理、类别级分类、实例级值标注和输出验证四个阶段；同时提出基于LLM的合成HTTP流量生成器，用于无需真实用户数据即可进行受控评估和示例提示。

Result: 在三种不同PII领域和粒度级别的分类法下，该流水线能够准确检测PII类型并提取相应的具体值。

Conclusion: LLM为灵活、与分类法无关的流量标注以及生成带标签数据提供了有前景的基础，尤其适用于隐私分类法不断演变的场景。

Abstract: Automated privacy audits of web and mobile applications often analyse outbound HTTP traffic to detect Personally Identifiable Information (PII) leakage. However, existing learning-based detectors typically depend on scarce, manually labelled traffic and are tightly coupled to fixed label taxonomies, limiting transferability across domains and evolving definitions of PII. This paper investigates whether Large Language Models (LLMs) can support taxonomy-agnostic annotation of explicitly transmitted PII values in HTTP message bodies when the taxonomy is provided at runtime. We introduce a multi-stage LLM-based pipeline that combines deterministic pre-processing with label-level classification, targeted instance-level value annotation, and output validation. To enable controlled evaluation and exemplar-based prompting without relying on sensitive real-user captures, we further propose an LLM-based generator for synthetic HTTP traffic with manually validated, taxonomy-derived PII annotations. We evaluate the approach across three taxonomies spanning different PII domains and granularity levels. Results show that the pipeline accurately detects PII types and extracts corresponding values for concrete PII taxonomies. Overall, our findings position LLMs as a promising foundation for flexible, taxonomy-agnostic traffic annotation and for creating labelled data under evolving privacy taxonomies.

</details>


### [145] [Mind the Gap? A Distributional Comparison of Real and Synthetic Priors for Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06343)
*Alex O. Davies,Telmo de Menezes e Silva Filho,Nirav Ajmeri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了三类表格基础模型预训练语料库（T4网页抓取、TabFM精选Kaggle、TabICL合成数据）的分布特性，发现合成数据仅覆盖真实表格空间的狭窄区域，且该差距无法通过超参数优化弥补；但出人意料的是，这种分布差异并未显著影响下游性能，表明覆盖真实数据分布并非TabICL泛化的主要驱动因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格基础模型的预训练数据来源（基准数据集、网页抓取、合成数据）对模型性能至关重要，但不同语料库之间的分布关系及其对下游任务的影响尚不明确，本文旨在填补这一认知空白。

Method: 选取三类代表性语料库（T4、TabFM、TabICL），通过全表、列和相关性层面的聚合特征进行表征，利用判别器AUC和k-NN覆盖度量进行比较，同时测试超参数优化（超过8.6万种配置）能否弥合合成数据与真实数据之间的分布差距。

Result: 合成语料库TabICL仅覆盖真实表格空间的狭窄区域；即使优化超参数也无法弥合该分布差距；精选Kaggle数据与网页抓取数据在分布层面大致可互换；分布差距未在特征邻近度或TabICL内部表征层面显著影响性能。

Conclusion: 覆盖真实数据分布并非TabICL泛化的主要驱动因素，合成数据的分布差距对性能影响有限，暗示表格基础模型的泛化机制可能依赖于其他因素。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models are pre-trained on one of three classes of corpus: curated datasets drawn from benchmark repositories, tables harvested at scale from the web, or synthetic tables sampled from a parametric generative prior. Despite the centrality of pre-training data to model performance, little is known about how these corpora relate to one another in distribution, and the impact this has on downstream performance. In this work we take three canonical, archetypal datasets used to train tabular foundation models; the T4 dataset represents web-scraped corpora, the TabFM dataset curated tables from Kaggle, and the TabICL dataset as the only well-used synthetic prior with publicly available parameters. We characterise each corpus using aggregate features over whole tables, columns and correlations, and compare them using discriminator AUCs and k-NN coverage metrics. We find that the TabICL synthetic prior occupies a narrow region of the space of real tables, that this mismatch cannot be closed by optimising prior hyper-parameters across more than 86 thousand configurations, and that curated and web-scraped corpora are broadly interchangeable on a distributional level in feature space. Surprisingly, the distributional gap between synthetic pre-training data and real tables has a clearly detectable effect on performance under neither feature-based proximity measures or TabICL's own internal representations, suggesting that coverage of the real-data distribution is not the primary driver of TabICL's generalisation.

</details>


### [146] [Prediction and Empowerment: A Theory of Agency through Bridge Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06346)
*Richard Csaky*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文在确定性物理/模拟世界中研究部分可观测下的智能体问题，证明预测、压缩和赋能三者之间存在分离关系。预测可通过识别隐藏商因子或覆盖控制实现，高赋能本身不足。在可细化接口和足够记忆条件下，动作条件化的观测压缩能减少潜在商因子的后验不确定性，并产生目标条件化的接口赋能。比特串特例中信息预算守恒使权衡显式化：预测需内部容量至少相关潜在熵，覆盖控制需终端动作容量。对现代AI智能体，目标应区分隐藏状态识别、接口细化、任务相关可控性和覆盖/干扰控制。人机对齐部分属于接口设计问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究确定性世界中部分可观测性下的智能体决策问题，澄清预测、压缩和赋能三者之间的理论关系，并为AI系统设计提供理论基础。

Method: 将感知和驱动建模为桥接接口，在确定性POMDP框架下进行分析，通过潜在微状态先验和多对一观测粗化建立模型，并引入比特串特例进行形式化分析。

Result: 证明了预测、压缩和赋能三者可分离；预测可通过识别隐藏商因子或覆盖控制实现；高赋能单独不足以实现预测；动作条件化的观测压缩可减少后验不确定性；信息预算守恒下，不同预测方式对容量需求不同。

Conclusion: 现代AI智能体的设计原则应区分隐藏状态识别、接口细化、任务相关可控性和覆盖/干扰控制；人机对齐部分属于接口设计问题。

Abstract: We study agency under partial observability in deterministic physical or simulated worlds, where apparent randomness arises from uncertainty over initial conditions, fixed law bits, and unrolled exogenous noise. We model sensing and actuation as bridge interfaces split between agent-controlled parameters and environment-controlled channel state, inducing a deterministic POMDP through a prior over latent microstates and many-to-one observation coarsening. Within this framework, we prove a separation between prediction, compression, and empowerment. Perfect prediction can be achieved either by identifying the hidden quotient relevant to the target family or by overwrite control that makes the future target action-determined; high empowerment alone is insufficient. Under refinable interfaces and sufficient memory, action-conditioned observation-compression progress reduces posterior uncertainty about the latent quotient, and when refinement requires steering world-side channel conditions, this creates target-conditioned interface empowerment. A bit-string specialization with a conserved information budget makes the resulting tradeoff explicit: prediction by identification requires internal capacity at least the relevant latent entropy, whereas overwrite control requires terminal action capacity over the controlled quotient. For modern AI agents, the results suggest a design principle rather than a theorem of inevitability: objectives should distinguish hidden-state identification, interface refinement, task-relevant controllability, and mere overwrite or distractor control. Human--AI alignment is partly an interface-design problem, where the relevant bridge is between human intent, agent internal state, external tools, and world-side channel conditions. This is a working draft: feedback and criticism is most welcome.

</details>


### [147] [From Agent Loops to Deterministic Graphs: Execution Lineage for Reproducible AI-Native Work](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06365)
*Josh Rosen,Seth Rosen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出执行谱系（execution lineage）模型，将AI原生工作表示为有向无环图（DAG）形式的工件产生计算，具有显式依赖、稳定中间边界和基于身份的重放能力。实验表明，DAG重放相比循环基线在无关分支更新中实现零污染，在中间工件编辑中实现完美的上游保留、下游传播和跨工件一致性，证明最终答案质量与维护状态质量是截然不同的指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型系统日益被部署为交织推理、工具使用、记忆和迭代精化的智能体工作流，但这些系统依赖隐式对话状态，难以保留稳定工作产品、隔离无关更新或通过中间工件传播变更。

Method: 提出执行谱系模型，将AI原生工作表示为有向无环图（DAG）形式的工件产生计算，具备显式依赖、稳定中间边界和基于身份的重放能力。

Result: 在无关分支更新任务中，DAG重放实现零污染和精确保留最终备忘录；在中间工件编辑任务中，所有系统都能反映新约束，但只有DAG重放实现完美的上游保留、下游传播、未受影响工件保留和跨工件一致性。

Conclusion: 最终答案质量与维护状态质量是截然不同的指标；强循环基线在有界合成/更新任务中能产生高质量最终输出，但可能掩盖部分状态不一致性，而执行谱系能提供更强的一致性保证。

Abstract: Large language model systems are increasingly deployed as agentic workflows that interleave reasoning, tool use, memory, and iterative refinement. These systems are effective at producing answers, but they often rely on implicit conversational state, making it difficult to preserve stable work products, isolate irrelevant updates, or propagate changes through intermediate artifacts.
  We introduce execution lineage: an execution model in which AI-native work is represented as a directed acyclic graph (DAG) of artifact-producing computations with explicit dependencies, stable intermediate boundaries, and identity-based replay. The goal is not to make the model a better one-shot writer, but to make evolving AI-generated work maintainable under change.
  We compare execution-lineage replay against loop-centric update baselines on two controlled policy-memo update tasks. In an unrelated-branch update, DAG replay preserved the final memo exactly in all runs, with zero churn and zero unrelated-branch contamination, while loop baselines regenerated the memo and frequently imported unrelated context. In an intermediate-artifact edit, all systems reflected the new constraint in the final memo, but only DAG replay achieved perfect upstream preservation, downstream propagation, unaffected-artifact preservation, and cross-artifact consistency.
  These results show that final answer quality and maintained-state quality are distinct. Strong loop baselines can remain competitive at producing polished final outputs when the task is a bounded synthesis/update problem and all current sources fit in context, but immediate task success can mask partial state inconsistency that may compound over future revisions. Execution lineage provides stronger guarantees about what should change, what should remain stable, and how work evolves across revisions.

</details>


### [148] [Rethinking Vacuity for OOD Detection in Evidential Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06382)
*Claire McNamara*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现基于 vacuity（不确定性质量）的OOD检测在证据深度学习中对类别基数K高度敏感。当ID和OOD的类别数不同时，AUROC和AUPR可被人为膨胀（AUROC最多差0.360，AUPR最多差0.683），这是一种评估假象。作者在EDL微调的大语言模型和MCQA数据集上进行了理论和实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前EDL中使用vacuity/UM作为OOD检测指标时，默认假设K与信念强度S之间存在线性关系，但实际中EDL会抑制错误证据，导致K与S并非线性相关。当ID和OOD的类别基数K不相等时，评估结果会被系统性扭曲，产生虚假的高性能表现。

Method: 首先从理论上分析vacuity公式对K的依赖性及其与S的非线性关系；然后通过实验对比标准EDL和IB-EDL在ID/OOD类别基数相差1时的AUROC和AUPR变化；最后扩展到因果语言模型的MCQA场景，讨论ID/OOD的明确定义问题。

Result: 当ID和OOD类别数相差1时，标准EDL的AUROC差异可达0.318，AUPR差异可达0.613；IB-EDL的AUROC差异可达0.360，AUPR差异可达0.683。这些差异是在模型预测不变的情况下仅由类别基数不同导致的人为膨胀。

Conclusion: 基于vacuity的OOD检测在EDL微调的LLM中对类别基数差异高度敏感，现有评估结果可能存在严重的评估假象。需要在ID和OOD类别基数严格相等的情况下进行评估，并对MCQA场景下的ID/OOD定义进行更清晰的规范。

Abstract: Vacuity, or Uncertainty Mass (UM), is commonly used as a metric to evaluate Out-of-Distribution (OOD) detection in Evidential Deep Learning (EDL). It generally involves dividing the number of classes ($K$) by the total strength of belief ($S$) of the model's predictions, where $S$ is derived from summing the Dirichlet parameters. As such, UM is sensitive to the cardinality of $K$. In particular, it is unlikely in practice that there is a linear relationship between $K$ and $S$ as $K$ and $S$ increase due to the nature of EDL (suppressing incorrectly assigned evidence). As a result, when comparing In Distribution (ID) and OOD results, it is important that $K_{\mathrm{ID}}$ and $K_{\mathrm{OOD}}$ are equal; something that is not always ensured in practice. We provide an empirical demonstration of how results for AUROC and AUPR can substantially differ when class cardinality between ID and OOD differs by 1, with AUROC differing by as much as 0.318 and AUPR by 0.613 for standard EDL, and AUROC by 0.360 and AUPR by 0.683 for IB-EDL. More concretely, our findings isolate an evaluation artefact: when K differs between ID and OOD, AUROC/AUPR can be artificially inflated without any change in model predictions. We further discuss the evaluation of EDL over causal language models using Multiple-Choice Question-Answer (MCQA) datasets and argue for clearer definitions of ID and OOD in this context. Our primary contribution is an empirical and theoretical demonstration that vacuity-based OOD detection in EDL-fine-tuned LLMs is highly sensitive to uncontrolled differences in evaluated class cardinality.

</details>


### [149] [Automated alignment is harder than you think](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06390)
*Aleksandr Bowkis,Marie Davidsen Buhl,Jacob Pfau,Geoffrey Irving*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 自动化对齐研究可能导致误导性安全评估，即使AI智能体无意破坏，也会因难以监督的模糊任务而产生系统性错误，最终意外部署有害AI。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对齐方案主张用AI智能体自动化对齐研究，但作者指出即使智能体没有恶意，这种方案仍可能因模糊任务中的系统性错误导致灾难性后果，因此需要分析其风险机制。

Method: 通过对比分析自动化对齐研究与人类对齐研究的差异，识别出四个关键风险因素：优化压力导致错误集中、非人类错误模式、不可评估的解决方案、以及输出相关性高。

Result: 自动化对齐研究比人类研究更容易产生误导性安全评估，原因包括：优化压力使错误集中在人类最不易察觉处；AI错误不同于人类错误；AI方案可能产生人类无法评估的论证；共享参数导致输出高度相关。

Conclusion: 需要训练智能体可靠地执行难以监督的模糊任务，但通用化和可扩展监督这两种主流方法在自动化对齐背景下都面临新挑战。

Abstract: A leading proposal for aligning artificial superintelligence (ASI) is to use AI agents to automate an increasing fraction of alignment research as capabilities improve. We argue that, even when research agents are not scheming to deliberately sabotage alignment work, this plan could produce compelling but catastrophically misleading safety assessments resulting in the unintentional deployment of misaligned AI. This could happen because alignment research involves many hard-to-supervise fuzzy tasks (tasks without clear evaluation criteria, for which human judgement is systematically flawed). Consequently, research outputs will contain systematic, undetected errors, and even correct outputs could be incorrectly aggregated into overconfident safety assessments. This problem is likely to be worse for automated alignment research than for human-generated alignment research for several reasons: 1) optimisation pressure means agent-generated mistakes are concentrated among those that human reviewers are least likely to catch; 2) agents are likely to produce errors that do not resemble human mistakes; 3) AI-generated alignment solutions may involve arguments humans cannot evaluate; and 4) shared weights, data and training processes may make AI outputs more correlated than human equivalents. Therefore, agents must be trained to reliably perform hard-to-supervise fuzzy tasks. Generalisation and scalable oversight are the leading candidates for achieving this but both face novel challenges in the context of automated alignment.

</details>


### [150] [Knowledge Graphs, the Missing Link in Agentic AI-based Formal Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06434)
*Vaisakh Naduvodi Viswambharan,Keerthan Kopparam Radhakrishna,Deepak Narayan Gadde,Aman Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于知识图谱的验证方法，通过从规范、RTL和形式化工具反馈中提取结构化中间表示来生成SystemVerilog断言，并利用语法修复、反例引导和覆盖导向三种循环机制提升断言质量和形式化验证覆盖率（78.5%-99.4%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM生成的断言存在规范到RTL关联薄弱、语义不匹配和语法错误频发的问题，因为规范常含歧义且关键微架构细节存在于RTL中，而现有方法将规范和RTL视为松散文本。

Method: 构建基于结构化中间表示的知识图谱，链接需求、设计层次、信号、假设和属性；采用多智能体工作流查询和更新知识图谱，驱动三种精化循环：语法诊断修复、反例引导修正和覆盖导向属性增强。

Result: 在七个基准设计上的评估表明，该方法改善了规范到RTL的关联，一致生成可编译断言且语法修复开销低，形式化覆盖率达78.5%-99.4%，但收敛性存在设计依赖性，复杂时序和算术推理仍具挑战。

Conclusion: 知识图谱能有效增强断言生成的规范-RTL关联性和语法正确性，但LLM在复杂时序和算术推理方面仍有局限。

Abstract: Recent advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) have enabled workflows that generate SystemVerilog Assertions (SVAs) from natural-language specifications, with the potential to accelerate Formal Verification (FV). However, high-quality assertion synthesis remains challenging because specifications are often ambiguous or incomplete and critical micro-architectural details reside in the Register Transfer Level (RTL). Many existing approaches treat the specification and RTL as loosely structured text, which weakens specification-to-RTL grounding and leads to semantic mismatches and frequent syntax failures during formal parsing and elaboration. This work addresses these limitations with a verification-centric Knowledge Graph (KG) constructed from structured Intermediate Representations (IRs) extracted from the specification, RTL, and formal-tool feedback, including syntax diagnostics, Counterexamples (CEXs), and coverage reports. The KG links requirements, design hierarchy, signals, assumptions, and properties to provide traceable, design-grounded context for generation. A multi-agent workflow queries and updates this KG to generate SVAs and to drive three refinement loops: syntax repair guided by tool diagnostics, CEX-guided correction using trace links, and coverage-directed property augmentation. Evaluation across seven benchmark designs indicates that KG-based context retrieval improves specification-to-RTL grounding and consistently produces compilable SVAs with low syntax-repair overhead. The approach achieves formal coverage ranging from 78.5% to 99.4%, though convergence exhibits design dependence with complex temporal and arithmetic reasoning remaining challenging for current LLM capabilities.

</details>


### [151] [SCRuB: Social Concept Reasoning under Rubric-Based Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06444)
*Jamelle Watson-Daniels,Himaghna Bhattacharjee,Skyler Wang,Brandon Handoko,Antonio Li,Anaelia Ovalle,Mahesh Pasupuleti,Candace Ross,Vidya Sarma,Arjun Subramonian,Karen Ullrich,Will van der Vaart,Yijing Xin,Maximilian Nickel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SCRuB框架，用于评估大语言模型在社交概念推理方面的能力。研究发现，前沿模型在所有五个推理维度上均超越人类专家，在80.8%的成对比较中被专家评委评为第一，74.4%的情况下模型回答更受青睐。研究首次证明，在单轮考试式评估中，社交概念推理已达到评估饱和——模型和人类均无法进一步提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注LLM在数学或技术任务上的推理能力，极少关注社交概念推理——即塑造社会规范、文化和制度的抽象思想。随着LLM作为社会智能体的应用日益广泛，评估其社交概念推理能力至关重要，但目前缺乏系统化的评估方法。

Method: SCRuB框架包含三个阶段：(1)从既定来源构建提示；(2)专家和模型分别生成回答；(3)使用五维批判性思维评分标准进行比较评估。引入"学科视角小组"集成方法，并基于独立专家评委进行验证。构建了SCRuBEval（4,711个评估提示）和SCRuBAnnotations（300个专家撰写的回答和150个专家比较判断，来自45位博士级学者）两个数据集。

Result: 前沿模型在所有五个评分维度上均超越人类专家。在1,170组成对比较中，专家评委在80.8%的判断中将模型回答排为第一，74.4%的情况下偏好模型回答。

Conclusion: 本研究首次基于专家验证证明了社交概念推理的评估饱和现象：单轮考试式评估格式已达到天花板，无论对模型还是人类而言都难以进一步提升。这表明需要开发更复杂的评估范式来继续推动该领域的发展。

Abstract: While many studies of Large Language Model (LLM) reasoning capabilities emphasize mathematical or technical tasks, few address reasoning about social concepts: the abstract ideas shaping social norms, culture, and institutions. This understudied capability is essential for modern models acting as social agents, yet no systematic evaluation methodology targets it. We introduce SCRuB (Social Concept Reasoning under Rubric-Based Evaluation), a framework designed for this setting of task indeterminacy. Our goal is to measure the degree to which a model reasons about social concepts with the depth and critical rigor of a human expert. SCRuB proceeds in three phases: prompt construction from established sources, response generation by experts and models, and comparative evaluation using a five-dimensional critical thinking rubric. To enable generalization of the pipeline, we introduce a Panel of Disciplinary Perspectives ensemble validated against independent expert judges. We release SCRuBEval (n=4,711 evaluation prompts) and SCRuBAnnotations (300 expert-authored responses and 150 expert comparative judgments from 45 PhD-level scholars). Our results show that frontier models consistently outperform human experts across all five rubric dimensions. Across 1,170 pairwise comparisons, expert judges ranked a model response first in 80.8% of judgments and preferred model responses overall 74.4% of the time. Ultimately, this study provides the first expert-grounded demonstration of evaluation saturation for social concept reasoning: the single-turn exam-style format has reached its ceiling for models and humans alike.

</details>


### [152] [ReasonSTL: Bridging Natural Language and Signal Temporal Logic via Tool-Augmented Process-Rewarded Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06483)
*Bowen Ye,Zhijian Li,Junyue Huang,Junkai Ma,Xiang Yin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ReasonSTL框架，通过工具增强和过程奖励训练，让本地开源小模型（4B参数）实现从自然语言到信号时序逻辑（STL）公式的高质量翻译，在自动指标和人工评估上达到SOTA，同时保证低成本和隐私安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自然语言到STL翻译面临三大挑战：手动编写需要时序逻辑专业知识且难以扩展；调用商业LLM API产生高昂token成本；将系统需求暴露给第三方服务存在隐私风险，不适合工业部署。

Method: ReasonSTL采用工具增强框架，将翻译过程分解为显式推理、确定性工具调用和结构化公式构建三个步骤；引入过程奖励训练（process-rewarded training）来监督工具使用轨迹和最终公式；同时构建了基于真实信号的双语、计算感知基准STL-Bench。

Result: 4B参数的模型在ReasonSTL训练下，在自动评估指标和人工评估中均达到最先进水平（SOTA）。

Conclusion: ReasonSTL为形式化规格草拟提供了透明、低成本且保护隐私的替代方案，证明通过合理框架设计，小模型也能在专业翻译任务上超越大模型。

Abstract: Signal Temporal Logic (STL) is an expressive formal language for specifying spatio-temporal requirements over real-valued, real-time signals. It has been widely used for the verification and synthesis of autonomous systems and cyber-physical systems. In practice, however, users often express their requirements in natural language rather than in structured STL formulas, making natural-language-to-STL translation a critical yet challenging task. Manual specification requires temporal-logic expertise and cannot scale, while prompting commercial LLM APIs incurs substantial token costs and may expose sensitive system requirements to third-party services, raising privacy concerns for industrial deployment. To address these challenges, we present \textsc{ReasonSTL}, a tool-augmented framework that adapts local open-source language models for natural-language-to-STL generation. \textsc{ReasonSTL} decomposes the translation process into explicit reasoning, deterministic tool calls, and structured formula construction. We further introduce process-rewarded training to supervise both tool-use trajectories and final formulas, together with \textsc{STL-Bench}, a bilingual, computation-aware benchmark grounded in real-world signals. Experiments show that a 4B model trained with \textsc{ReasonSTL} achieves state-of-the-art performance in both automatic metrics and human evaluations, demonstrating that \textsc{ReasonSTL} provides a transparent, low-cost, and privacy-preserving alternative for formal specification drafting.

</details>


### [153] [From Token Lists to Graph Motifs: Weisfeiler-Lehman Analysis of Sparse Autoencoder Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06494)
*Ruben Fernandez-Boullon,Pablo Magariños-Docampo,Javier Perez-Robles*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于图结构的稀疏自编码器（SAE）特征表示方法，将每个SAE特征建模为token共现图，并通过自定义WL式频率分箱图核计算相似度进行聚类。实验表明，该方法能发现解码器余弦相似度聚类无法获取的启发式主题族（如标点模式、语言脚本簇、代码模板），图视图与token直方图基线互补，且聚类结果对超参数稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SAE特征分析几乎完全依赖top激活token列表或解码器权重向量，忽略了特征间高阶共现结构。作者希望通过图结构表示揭示未被探索的特征共现关系，提供互补性分析视角。

Method: 将每个SAE特征建模为token共现图：节点为强激活附近最频繁的token，边连接局部上下文窗口内共现的token对；设计自定义WL-style频率分箱图核计算图间相似度；在GPT-2 Small的SAE特征上使用合成混合领域语料进行聚类分析。

Result: 聚类恢复了启发式主题族（标点密集模式、语言/脚本簇、代码模板），这些是解码器余弦相似度聚类无法获得的；token直方图基线在整体纯度上更高，图视图贡献为互补而非主导；聚类结果对图构建超参数和随机种子稳定。

Conclusion: 图结构表示能揭示token频率和解码器权重视图无法捕获的结构关系，为SAE特征分析提供了有价值的互补视角。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) have become central to mechanistic interpretability, decomposing transformer activations into monosemantic features. Yet existing analyses characterise features almost exclusively through top-activating token lists or decoder weight vectors, leaving the higher-order co-occurrence structure shared across features largely unexamined. We introduce a graph-structured representation in which each SAE feature is modelled as a token co-occurrence graph: nodes are the tokens most frequent near strong activations, and edges connect pairs that co-occur within local context windows. A custom WL-style, frequency-binned graph kernel then provides a similarity measure over this structural space. Applied as a proof of concept to features from a large SAE trained on GPT-2 Small and probed with a synthetic mixed-domain corpus, our clustering recovers heuristic motif families (punctuation-heavy patterns, language and script clusters, and code-like templates) that are not recovered by clustering on decoder cosine similarity. A token-histogram baseline achieves higher overall purity, so the contribution of the graph view is complementary rather than dominant: it surfaces structural relationships that token-frequency and decoder-weight views alone do not capture. Cluster assignments are stable across graph-construction hyperparameters and random seeds.

</details>


### [154] [SpatialEpiBench: Benchmarking Spatial Information and Epidemic Priors in Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06530)
*Ruiqi Lyu,Alistair Turcan,Bryan Wilder*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SpatialEpiBench是一个用于时空流行病预测的标准基准，包含11个数据集和滚动评估方法。研究发现大多数现有方法在1天到1个月的预测范围内甚至不如简单的"last-value"基线，并识别出三大失败模式：爆发预测能力差、难以处理稀疏和噪声数据、地理邻近性对流行病空间信息效用有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管时空方法在流行病预测中备受关注，但缺乏标准化基准，现有评估常使用简单的时序训练-测试划分，无法反映实时预测实践。作者旨在填补这一空白，建立更贴近真实公共卫生场景的评估框架。

Method: 构建了包含11个流行病数据集的SpatialEpiBench基准，采用标准化的滚动评估（rolling evaluations）和爆发特定指标（outbreak-specific metrics）。评估了基于邻接信息的预测模型，并结合广泛使用的流行病先验（epidemic priors）将通用模型适配到流行病学领域。

Result: 大多数方法在1天到1个月的预测中表现不如简单的last-value基线（即使结合了流行病先验）。识别出三大失败模式：爆发预测能力差、对稀疏和噪声数据处理困难、地理邻接性作为流行病空间信息的效用有限。

Conclusion: 现有时空流行病预测模型在实际场景中表现不佳，需要开发更具操作实用性的预测模型。论文公开了基准数据、代码和说明以支持该领域发展。

Abstract: Accurate epidemic forecasting is crucial for public health response, resource allocation, and outbreak intervention, but remains difficult with sparse, noisy, and highly non-stationary data. Because epidemics unfold across interacting regions, spatiotemporal methods are natural candidates for improving forecasts. Despite growing interest in spatial information, no standardized benchmark exists, and current evaluations often use simple chronological train-test splits that do not reflect real-time forecasting practice. We address this gap with SpatialEpiBench, a challenging benchmark for spatiotemporal epidemic forecasting in realistic public-health settings. SpatialEpiBench includes 11 epidemic datasets with standardized rolling evaluations and outbreak-specific metrics. We evaluate adjacency-informed forecasting models with widely used epidemic priors that adapt general models to epidemiology, but find that most methods underperform a simple last-value baseline from 1 day to 1 month ahead, even during outbreaks and with these priors. We identify three major failure modes: (1) poor outbreak anticipation, (2) difficulty handling sparsity and noise, and (3) limited utility of common geographic adjacency for epidemiological spatial information. We release benchmark data, code, and instructions at https://github.com/Rachel-Lyu/SpatialEpiBench to support development of operationally useful epidemic forecasting models.

</details>


### [155] [Ex Ante Evaluation of AI-Induced Idea Diversity Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06540)
*Nafis Saami Azad,Raiyan Abdul Baten*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种评估AI创意系统导致人类多样性崩溃的框架，通过比较AI生成与人类基线作品在种群层面上的同质化程度，使用拥挤系数Δ和多样性比率ρ来衡量，并发现前沿LLM在多个任务中均低于多样性保持的临界值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI创意系统的评估仅关注个体效用，忽视了创意产出在种群层面消费时，同质化会导致价值贬损的问题，存在评估盲区。

Method: 提出了无需人机交互数据的人类相对框架，通过将创意视为可拥挤资源，利用分布内比较识别源级拥挤，定义了超额拥挤系数Δ和人类相对多样性比率ρ，并建立了ρ≥1的无超额拥挤条件，同时将其与曝光依赖的重叠成本的采纳博弈联系起来。

Result: 在短故事、营销标语和替代用途任务中，三个前沿LLM均低于多样性保持的临界值ρ≥1；估计量在可行的小样本量下保持稳定；通过针对性生成协议设计可以降低拥挤度。

Conclusion: 多样性崩溃是可操作的、可在开发阶段评估的优化目标，通过精心设计生成协议可以有效缓解AI带来的种群级同质化问题。

Abstract: Creative AI systems are typically evaluated at the level of individual utility, yet creative outputs are consumed in populations: an idea loses value when many others produce similar ones. This creates an evaluation blind spot, as AI can improve individual outputs while increasing population-level crowding. We introduce a human-relative framework for benchmarking AI-induced human diversity collapse without requiring human-AI interaction data, providing an ex ante protocol to estimate crowding risk from model-only generations and matched unaided human baselines. By modeling ideas as congestible resources, we show that source-level crowding is identifiable from within-distribution comparisons, yielding an excess-crowding coefficient $Δ$ and a human-relative diversity ratio $ρ$. We show that $ρ\ge1$ is the no-excess-crowding parity condition and connect $Δ$ to an adoption game with exposure-dependent redundancy costs. Across short stories, marketing slogans, and alternative-uses tasks, three frontier LLMs fall below parity across crowding kernels. Estimates stabilize with feasible model-only sample sizes. Importantly, generation-protocol variants show that crowding can be reduced through targeted design, making diversity collapse an actionable, development-time evaluation target for population-aware creative AI.

</details>


### [156] [Improved techniques for fine-tuning flow models via adjoint matching: a deterministic control pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06583)
*Zhengyi Guo,Jiayuan Sheng,David D. Yao,Wenpin Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种确定性伴随匹配框架，将流式生成模型的人类偏好对齐形式化为速度场上的最优控制问题，通过截断伴随方案和灵活的KL正则化扩展，在保持对齐质量的同时大幅提升计算效率和生成多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于流式生成模型的偏好对齐方法在计算效率和灵活性上存在局限，亟需一种既能高效对齐人类偏好又能保持生成多样性的理论框架。

Method: 1) 将偏好对齐建模为速度场上的最优控制问题，直接回归控制到当前策略下的值梯度诱导目标；2) 提出截断伴随方案，集中计算在轨迹末端奖励相关信号密集的区域；3) 将框架推广到标准KL正则化之外，支持对齐强度与分布保留间的灵活权衡。

Result: 在SiT-XL/2和FLUX.2-Klein-4B模型上，多个对齐指标取得一致性提升，同时生成多样性和模式保留显著改善。

Conclusion: 该框架在理论上统一了偏好对齐与最优控制，在实践上实现了高效、灵活且高保真的对齐效果，为流式生成模型的对齐训练提供了新范式。

Abstract: We propose a deterministic adjoint matching framework that formulates human preference alignment for flow-based generative models as an optimal control problem over velocity fields. One can directly regress the control toward a value-gradient-induced target under the current policy, leading to a simple and stable training objective. Building on this perspective, we introduce a truncated adjoint scheme that focuses computation on the terminal portion of the trajectory, where reward-relevant signals concentrate, which yields substantial computational savings while preserving alignment quality. We further generalize the framework beyond standard KL-based regularization, allowing more flexible trade-offs between alignment strength and distributional preservation. Experiments on SiT-XL/2 and FLUX.2-Klein-4B demonstrate consistent gains across multiple alignment metrics, along with substantially improved diversity and mode preservation.

</details>


### [157] [NeuroAgent: LLM Agents for Multimodal Neuroimaging Analysis and Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06584)
*Lujia Zhong,Yihao Xia,Jianwei Zhang,Shuo huang,Jiaxin Yue,Mingyang Xia,Yonggang Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: NeuroAgent是一个基于LLM的多智能体框架，能够自动化处理多模态神经影像数据（sMRI, fMRI, dMRI, PET）的预处理和分析流程，在ADNI数据集（1470名被试）上实现84.8%的预处理步骤正确率，并通过四模态融合在阿尔茨海默病分类中达到0.9518的AUC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态神经影像分析面临复杂且模态特定的预处理流程、异构工具链的协调困难，以及下游统计分析需要大量任务特定代码和评估协议等问题，导致从原始数据到可复现科学分析之间存在巨大障碍。

Method: 采用层级式多智能体架构，包含反馈驱动的"生成-执行-验证"引擎：智能体自主生成可执行的预处理代码，检测并从运行时错误中恢复，验证输出完整性。支持人工介入（Human-In-The-Loop）处理边缘情况。

Result: 在ADNI全部阶段的1470名被试（CN=1000, AD=470）上评估，最强后端Qwen3.5-27B达到84.8%端到端预处理步骤正确率，意图解析准确率最高达100%。四模态融合的阿尔茨海默病分类AUC达0.9518，优于所有单模态基线。

Conclusion: NeuroAgent能够显著减少神经影像预处理所需的人工投入，实现端到端的自动化分析流程，为神经影像学研究提供了可复现的自动化解决方案。

Abstract: Multimodal neuroimaging analysis often involves complex, modality-specific preprocessing workflows that require careful configuration, quality control, and coordination across heterogeneous toolchains. Beyond preprocessing, downstream statistical analysis and disease classification commonly require task-specific code, evaluation protocols, and data-format conventions, creating additional barriers between raw acquisitions and reproducible scientific analysis. We present NeuroAgent, an LLM-driven agentic framework that automates key preprocessing and analysis steps for heterogeneous neuroimaging data, including sMRI, fMRI, dMRI, and PET, and supports interactive downstream analysis through natural-language queries. NeuroAgent employs a hierarchical multi-agent architecture with a feedback-driven Generate-Execute-Validate engine: agents autonomously generate executable preprocessing code, detect and recover from runtime errors, and validate output integrity. We evaluate the system on 1,470 subjects pooled across all ADNI phases (CN=1,000, AD=470), where all subjects have sMRI and tabular data, with subsets also having Tau-PET (n=469), fMRI (n=278), and DTI ($n=620$). Pipeline ablation studies across multiple LLM backends show that capable models reach up to 100% intent-parsing accuracy, with the strongest backend (Qwen3.5-27B) reaching 84.8% end-to-end preprocessing step correctness. Automated recovery limits manual intervention to edge cases where human review is required via the Human-In-The-Loop interface. For Alzheimer's Disease classification using automatically preprocessed multimodal data, our agent ensemble achieves an AUC of 0.9518 with four modalities, outperforming all single-modality baselines. These results show that NeuroAgent can reduce the manual effort required for neuroimaging preprocessing and enable end-to-end automated analysis pipelines for neuroimaging research.

</details>


### [158] [GlazyBench: A Benchmark for Ceramic Glaze Property Prediction and Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06641)
*Ziyu Zhai,Siyou Li,Juexi Shao,Juntao Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了GlazyBench，首个用于AI辅助陶瓷釉料设计的数据集，包含23,148个真实配方，支持表面属性预测和视觉生成两项任务，并建立了全面的基线基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 陶瓷釉料开发依赖昂贵且耗时的试错过程，对独立艺术家造成沉重负担。尽管多模态AI提供了现代解决方案，但该领域缺乏训练这些模型所需的大规模数据集。

Method: 构建了包含23,148个真实釉料配方的GlazyBench数据集，定义了两个核心任务：从原材料预测烧成后表面属性（颜色、透明度等），以及基于属性生成釉料视觉表示。使用传统机器学习和大型语言模型建立属性预测基线，利用深度生成模型和多模态大模型建立图像生成基线。

Result: 实验结果表明了有前景但具有挑战性的结果，验证了数据集的可用性和任务的难度。

Conclusion: GlazyBench开创了AI辅助材料设计的新研究方向，为该领域的系统评估提供了标准化基准。

Abstract: Developing ceramic glazes is a costly, time-consuming process of trial and error due to complex chemistry, placing a significant burden on independent artists. While recent advances in multimodal AI offer a modern solution, the field lacks the large-scale datasets required to train these models. We propose GlazyBench, the first dataset for AI-assisted glaze design. Comprising 23,148 real glaze formulations, GlazyBench supports two primary tasks: predicting post-firing surface properties, such as color and transparency, from raw materials, and generating accurate visual representations of the glaze based on these properties. We establish comprehensive baselines for property prediction using traditional machine learning and large language models, alongside image generation benchmarks using deep generative and large multimodal models. Our experiments demonstrate promising yet challenging results. GlazyBench pioneers a new research direction in AI-assisted material design, providing a standardized benchmark for systematic evaluation.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [159] [Nationwide EHR-Based Chronic Rhinosinusitis Prediction Using Demographic-Stratified Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05213)
*Sicong Chang,Yidan Shen,Justina Varghese,Akshay R Prabhakar,Sebastian Guadarrama-Sistos-Vazquez,Jiefu Chen,Masayoshi Takashima,Omar G. Ahmed,Renjie Hu,Xin Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究利用《All of Us》项目的全国性纵向EHR数据，通过混合特征选择管道和人口统计学分层模型，基于诊断前两年的编码数据预测慢性鼻窦炎（CRS），总体AUC达0.8461，较基线提高0.0168，证明常规EHR数据可用于人群代表性的CRS风险分层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CRS是一种常见且异质性高的炎症性疾病，早期识别困难，症状与过敏性鼻炎等重叠，且不同表型使风险模式难以捕捉。现有预测研究多依赖单机构队列，缺乏人群层面的泛化能力。

Method: 使用《All of Us》全国纵向EHR数据；采用混合特征选择管道（先基于患病率统计筛选，再基于模型重要性排序），将约11万个候选编码压缩为100个可解释特征；针对六个性别和生命阶段亚组训练分层模型，并分别进行超参数调优。

Result: 总体AUC为0.8461，比最佳基线模型提升0.0168。

Conclusion: 常规收集的EHR数据可支持具有人群代表性的CRS风险分层，有助于初级保健中的早期分诊和转诊优先级判定。

Abstract: Chronic rhinosinusitis (CRS) is a common heterogeneous inflammatory disorder that causes substantial morbidity and healthcare costs. CRS is difficult to identify early from routine encounters, as symptom presentations overlap with common conditions such as allergic rhinitis, and heterogeneous phenotypes further obscure risk patterns. Prior predictive studies often rely on single-institutional cohorts , which reduce population-level generalizability. To overcome this, we leveraged nationwide longitudinal EHR data from the \textit{All of Us} Research Program to predict CRS diagnosis using two years of pre-diagnostic history. To address extreme feature sparsity and dimensionality in coded EHR data, we implemented a hybrid feature-selection pipeline that combines prevalence-based statistical screening with model-based importance ranking, compressing approximately 110,000 candidate codes into 100 interpretable features. To capture demographic heterogeneity, we trained demographic stratified models across six adult sex and life-stage subgroups with subgroup-specific hyperparameter tuning. Our framework achieved an overall AUC of 0.8461, improving discrimination by 0.0168 over the best baseline. These results demonstrate that routinely collected EHR data may support population-representative CRS risk stratification and inform earlier triage and referral prioritization in primary care.

</details>


### [160] [SAT: Sequential Agent Tuning for Coordinator Free Plug and Play Multi-LLM Training with Monotonic Improvement Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05216)
*Yi Xie,Yangyang Xu,Yi Fan,Bo Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出顺序智能体微调（SAT），一种无协调器的训练范式，通过因子化策略和块坐标更新使多个小型LLM组成的团队能够协同训练，实现单调改进和即插即用保证。3个4B智能体团队（总参数量12B）在AIME24/25基准上超越Qwen3-32B模型3.9%，且可无重训练地替换更强智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型虽性能强大但部署成本高昂，现有工作尝试使用多个小型、高效LLM组成的团队来匹敌或超越单个大模型。然而，联合更新多个智能体会引入复合分布偏移，导致训练过程中的协调和稳定性困难。

Method: 提出顺序智能体微调（SAT），是一种无协调器的训练范式。将团队表示为因子化策略，对智能体执行块坐标更新，实现可扩展的分散式训练。具体包括：序列感知的on-policy优势估计器（条件于演化中的团队策略），以及每个智能体的KL信任域（隔离占据漂移）。理论上保证单调改进和可证明的即插即用不变性。

Result: 3个4B智能体团队（总参数量12B）在AIME24/25基准上平均超越Qwen3-32B模型3.9%。通过即插即用替换两个8B智能体后，综合分数提升10.4%。

Conclusion: SAT框架通过理论保证（单调改进和即插即用不变性）和实证验证，表明多智能体协作训练可在不增加总参数量的情况下超越大模型，且支持灵活升级单个智能体而无需重训练整个团队。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) with a large number of parameters achieve strong performance but are often prohibitively expensive to deploy. Recent work explores using teams of smaller, more efficient LLMs that collectively match or even outperform a single large model. However, jointly updating multiple agents introduces compounding distribution shifts, making coordination and stability during training difficult. We address this by introducing Sequential Agent Tuning (SAT), a coordinator-free training paradigm. SAT represents the team as a factorized policy and employs block-coordinate updates over agents, enabling scalable, decentralized training without a central controller. Specifically, we develop a sequence-aware, on-policy advantage estimator that conditions on the evolving team policy, coupled with per-agent KL trust regions that isolate occupancy drift. Theoretically, this framework provides two critical guarantees. First, it ensures monotonic improvement, stabilizing the training process. Second, it establishes provable plug-and-play invariance: any agent can be upgraded to a stronger model without retraining the rest of the team, with a formal guarantee that the performance bound improves. Empirically, a team of three 4B agents (12B total) trained with SAT surpasses the much larger Qwen3-32B on AIME24/25 benchmarks by 3.9\% on average. We validate our plug-and-play theory by swapping in two 8B agents, which boosts the composite score by 10.4\%. We provide code and appendix of proof at https://github.com/Yydc/SAT-AAMAS

</details>


### [161] [Horizon-Constrained Rashomon Sets for Chaotic Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05218)
*Gauri Kale,Rahul Vishwakarma,Holly Diamond,Ava Hedayatipour,Amin Rezaei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了horizon-constrained Rashomon sets框架，建立了混沌理论与预测多重性之间的首次严格联系。该框架刻画了混沌系统中预测模型随预测时域演化的多样性，证明了有效Rashomon集随提前时间呈指数收缩（收缩率由最大Lyapunov指数决定），并引入Lyapunov加权指标以更紧地界定预测分歧。基于此，开发了面向决策的对齐选择算法，在Lorenz-96、Kuramoto-Sivashinsky等混沌系统及风电、交通、天气等真实应用中，决策质量提升18-34%，同时保持竞争性预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测多重性（predictive multiplicity）与混沌动力学（chaotic dynamics）是机器学习中两个独立发展的基本挑战，尽管它们存在概念上的联系。现有工作尚未在混沌系统背景下系统研究预测模型的多样性演化问题，缺乏将两者统一的理论框架。

Method: 1) 提出horizon-constrained Rashomon sets理论框架，刻画模型多样性随预测时域的变化；2) 证明混沌系统中有效Rashomon集以最大Lyapunov指数决定的速率指数收缩；3) 引入Lyapunov加权指标，为预测分歧提供更紧的边界；4) 开发基于下游效用的决策对齐模型选择算法。

Result: 在Lorenz-96、Kuramoto-Sivashinsky等合成混沌系统以及风电、交通、天气等真实应用中，所提方法在保持竞争性预测性能的同时，决策质量提升18-34%。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了混沌理论与预测多重性之间的首次严格理论联系，为在安全关键的混沌领域部署机器学习模型提供了原则性指导。

Abstract: Predictive multiplicity and chaotic dynamics represent two fundamental challenges in machine learning that have evolved independently despite their conceptual connections. We bridge this gap by introducing horizon-constrained Rashomon sets, a theoretical framework that characterizes how model multiplicity evolves with prediction horizon in chaotic systems. Unlike static prediction tasks where the Rashomon set remains fixed, chaos induces exponential divergence among initially similar models, fundamentally transforming the nature of predictive equivalence. We prove that the effective Rashomon set contracts exponentially with lead time at a rate determined by the maximum Lyapunov exponent and introduce Lyapunov-weighted metrics that provide tighter bounds on predictive disagreement. Leveraging these insights, we develop decision-aligned selection algorithms that choose among near-optimal models based on downstream utility rather than forecast accuracy alone. Extensive experiments on synthetic chaotic systems (Lorenz-96, Kuramoto-Sivashinsky) and real-world applications (wind power, traffic, weather) demonstrate that our framework improves decision quality by 18-34\% while maintaining competitive predictive performance. This work establishes the first rigorous connection between chaos theory and predictive multiplicity, providing principled guidance for deploying machine learning in safety-critical chaotic domains.

</details>


### [162] [MidSteer: Optimal Affine Framework for Steering Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05220)
*Tatiana Gaintseva,Andrew Stepanov,Ziquan Liu,Martin Benning,Gregory Slabaugh,Jiankang Deng,Ismail Elezi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文形式化了概念引导（concept steering）的理论框架，证明了标准引导方法与LEACE（仿射概念擦除）之间的关联，提出了LEACE-Switch（概念切换）和MidSteer（最小扰动概念引导）两种仿射操作方法，并在多种模型架构上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管概念引导在生成模型的后部署对齐和安全设置中取得了经验成功，但目前缺乏一个全面的理论框架来统一理解其工作原理。

Method: 1) 建立了概念引导与仿射概念擦除（LEACE）之间的理论联系；2) 形式化了概念切换框架LEACE-Switch；3) 提出了更通用的MidSteer框架，放松了假设条件，支持有向、最小扰动的概念变换。

Result: MidSteer在多种任务、模态和架构（包括视觉扩散模型和大语言模型）上表现出良好的性能。

Conclusion: 通过形式化理论框架和提出MidSteer方法，为概念引导提供了理论基础和更通用的解决方案，在多种生成模型上有效。

Abstract: Steering intermediate representations has emerged as a powerful strategy for controlling generative models, particularly in post-deployment alignment and safety settings. However, despite its empirical success, it currently lacks a comprehensive theoretical framework. In this paper, we bridge this gap by formalizing the theory of concept steering. First, we establish a link between steering and affine concept erasure, proving that the standard approach for removing unwanted behaviors is a special case of LEACE (a closed-form method for affine erasure). Next, we formulate a principled theoretical framework for concept switching, LEACE-Switch, and characterize the assumptions under which it provides an optimal affine solution. Building on this analysis, we then introduce MidSteer (Minimal Disturbance concept Steering), a more general affine framework for concept manipulation that relaxes these assumptions and enables directed, minimal-disturbance transformations. We demonstrate that MidSteer performs favorably across a range of tasks, modalities, and architectures, including vision diffusion models and large language models.

</details>


### [163] [Adaptive Computation Depth via Learned Token Routing in Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05222)
*Ahmed Abdelmuniem Abdalla Mohammed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Token-Selective Attention (TSA)，一种在Transformer块间残差更新上学习每个token的门控机制，通过轻量级MLP生成连续停止概率，实现端到端可微且仅增加1.7%参数。TSA能按难度自适应跳过20%的token-层操作，在字符级语言建模中节省14-23%计算量，质量损失<0.5%，且学习到的路由可直接用于推理时稀疏执行加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Transformer对所有token应用相同层数，未考虑上下文难度差异，导致计算浪费。本文旨在通过可学习的门控机制实现按token难度动态分配计算资源。

Method: 在相邻Transformer块的残差更新之间，为每个token学习一个门控值。该门控由轻量级两层MLP生成连续的停止概率，使整个机制端到端可微。训练时无需显式深度正则化（λ=0），任务损失梯度即可驱动路由器跳过约20%的操作。

Result: 在字符级语言建模任务中，Tiny-Shakespeare和enwik8上节省14-23%的token-层操作，质量损失小于0.5%。在同等效率下，比early exit方法验证损失低0.7%。学习到的路由可直接用于推理时的稀疏执行，实现实际加速。

Conclusion: TSA是一种高效、轻量的动态计算分配方法，能在不改变基础架构的情况下按token难度自适应跳过计算，且训练与推理一致，具有实际加速效果。

Abstract: Standard transformer architectures apply the same number of layers to every token regardless of contextual difficulty. We present Token-Selective Attention (TSA), a learned per-token gate on residual updates between consecutive transformer blocks. Each gate is a lightweight two-layer multi-layer perceptron (MLP) that produces a continuous halting probability, making the mechanism end-to-end differentiable with 1.7% parameter overhead and no changes to the base architecture. Notably, TSA learns difficulty-proportional routing without any explicit depth pressure: even at $λ=0$ (no depth regularisation), the task-loss gradient alone drives the router to skip 20% of token-layer operations. On character-level language modeling, TSA saved 14-23% of token-layer operations (TLOps) across Tiny-Shakespeare and enwik8 at <0.5% quality loss. At matched efficiency, TSA achieved 0.7% lower validation loss than early exit, and the learned routing transfers directly to inference-time sparse execution for real wall-clock speedup.

</details>


### [164] [Structural Instability of Feature Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05223)
*Yunpeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了稀疏自编码器（SAE）中特征组合激活的几何不稳定性，在高维稀疏锥流形模型下推导了组合坍塌阈值，并揭示了ReLU整流导致的棘轮效应，在CLEVR数据集上验证了预测的缩放趋势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管稀疏自编码器已被用于解耦transformer架构中的特征叠加并实现激活控制，但组合式激活（同时激活多个语义隐变量）的理论基础尚未被充分探索，线性表示假说往往忽略了过完备字典中的非线性干扰效应。

Method: 将激活空间建模为高维稀疏锥流形，在球形字典模型下利用高斯平均宽度（统计维度）推导组合坍塌的渐近阈值；分析高偏置下ReLU整流如何将微观相关诱导的方差波动转化为系统性漂移，产生类棘轮效应的干扰增长。

Result: 在CLEVR数据集上的结构化语义特征验证了预测的缩放趋势，表明层级相关性会加速相对于随机基线的转化过程。

Conclusion: 研究结果揭示了基于组合的激活操控在可扩展性上受到几何约束，并激励设计能够显式管理干扰而非依赖朴素线性叠加的组合机制。

Abstract: Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) have emerged as a powerful paradigm for disentangling feature superposition in transformer-based architectures, enabling precise control via activation steering. However, the theoretical foundations of compositional steering -- the simultaneous activation of distinct semantic latents -- remain under-explored. The prevailing Linear Representation Hypothesis often abstracts away non-linear interference effects that arise in overcomplete dictionaries. We present a geometric framework for analyzing the instability of feature unions. Modeling the activation space as a high-dimensional sparse cone manifold, we derive an asymptotic compositional-collapse threshold under a spherical dictionary model, characterized by the Gaussian mean width (statistical dimension) of the signal cone. We further show that, in the high-bias regime, ReLU rectification converts microscopic correlation-induced variance fluctuations into a systematic drift that accumulates under composition, yielding interference growth consistent with a ratchet effect. We validate the predicted scaling trends on structured semantic features extracted from CLEVR, where hierarchical correlations accelerate the transition relative to random baselines. Together, our results highlight geometric constraints on the scalability of union-based steering and motivate composition mechanisms that explicitly manage interference beyond naive linear superposition.

</details>


### [165] [Channel-Level Semantic Perturbations: Unlearnable Examples for Diverse Training Paradigms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05224)
*Bo Wang,Jia Ni,Mengnan Zhao,Zhan Qin,Kui Ren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究不可学习示例在预训练-微调范式下的表现，发现冻结预训练权重会削弱现有UE方法的效果，并提出层次化欺骗策略SSC来保持数据不可学习性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有不可学习示例研究主要关注从头训练场景，忽略了预训练-微调这一广泛采用的训练范式，导致其在更实际场景下的防御效果未知。

Method: 通过实验揭示冻结浅层预训练权重会保留数据语义从而过滤不可学习噪声；提出Shallow Semantic Camouflage (SSC)策略，将扰动生成限制在语义有效子空间以绕过预训练权重的语义抑制。

Result: SSC在浅层冻结和语义聚焦预训练等挑战性范式下持续保持数据的不可学习性。

Conclusion: 本研究填补了基于预训练的不可学习学习的关键空白，证明了针对预训练-微调范式设计不可学习示例的必要性和可行性。

Abstract: The unauthorized use of personal data in model training has emerged as a growing privacy threat. Unlearnable examples (UEs) address this issue by embedding imperceptible perturbations into benign examples to obstruct feature learning. However, existing studies mainly evaluate UEs under from-scratch training settings, leaving their behavior under the widely adopted pretraining-finetuning (PF) paradigm largely unexplored. In this work, we provide the first systematic investigation of unlearnable examples across diverse training paradigms. Our analysis reveals that loading and freezing pretrained weights significantly weakens the effectiveness of existing UEs methods. We further explain these findings through semantic filtering: while UEs tend to induce models to overfit non-semantic noise, thereby weakening their semantic extraction capabilities, under the PF paradigm, frozen shallow layers preserve data semantics, effectively filtering out distracting information like unlearnable noise. Guided by these insights, we propose a hierarchical deception strategy, Shallow Semantic Camouflage (SSC), that confines the generation process to a semantically valid subspace, aiming to bypass the semantic suppression introduced by pretrained weights. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method consistently preserves data unlearnability even under challenging training paradigms, such as shallow-layer freezing and semantic-focused pretraining (SF-Pretrain), bridging the critical gap in pretrain-based unlearnable learning.

</details>


### [166] [MACS: Modality-Aware Capacity Scaling for Efficient Multimodal MoE Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05225)
*Bo Li,Chuan Wu,shaolin Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对MoE多模态大模型在专家并行推理中的效率瓶颈（拖尾效应），提出了MACS——一种无需训练、基于模态感知的容量缩放推理框架。通过熵加权负载机制量化视觉token语义价值，结合动态模态自适应容量机制根据输入实时模态分配专家资源，显著提升了多模态基准测试的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE多模态大模型在专家并行推理中存在严重的效率瓶颈（拖尾效应），而现有基于token计数的负载均衡方法无法解决多模态场景下的两个独特挑战：信息异质性（冗余视觉token与关键token被同等对待）和模态动态性（不同任务中视觉与文本比例变化导致资源错配）。

Method: 提出MACS框架：1) 熵加权负载机制（Entropy-Weighted Load），通过熵值量化视觉token的语义价值以解决信息异质性；2) 动态模态自适应容量机制（Dynamic Modality-Adaptive Capacity），根据输入实时模态组成分配专家资源。

Result: 在多模态基准测试上显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: MACS为MoE多模态大模型在专家并行推理中的高效部署提供了一种新颖且鲁棒的解决方案，且无需额外训练。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts Multimodal Large Language Models (MoE MLLMs) suffer from a significant efficiency bottleneck during Expert Parallelism (EP) inference due to the straggler effect. This issue is worsened in the multimodal context, as existing token-count-based load balancing methods fail to address two unique challenges: (1) Information Heterogeneity, where numerous redundant visual tokens are treated equally to semantically critical ones, and (2) Modality Dynamics, where varying visual to text ratios across tasks lead to resource misallocation. To address these challenges, we propose MACS (Modality-Aware Capacity Scaling), a training-free inference framework. Specifically, MACS introduces an Entropy-Weighted Load mechanism to quantify the semantic value of visual tokens, addressing information heterogeneity. Additionally, the Dynamic Modality-Adaptive Capacity mechanism allocates expert resources based on the real-time modal composition of the input. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MACS significantly outperforms existing methods on various multimodal benchmarks, providing a novel and robust solution for the efficient deployment of MoE MLLMs in EP inference.

</details>


### [167] [Internalizing Outcome Supervision into Process Supervision: A New Paradigm for Reinforcement Learning for Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05226)
*Fei Ding,Yongkang Zhang,Runhao Liu,Yuhao Liao,Zijian Zeng,Sibo wang,Huiming Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种监督内化方法，将结果层面的监督信号转化为过程层面的学习信号，使模型在仅有结果监督的强化学习训练中自动识别、纠正和复用失败推理轨迹，实现细粒度策略优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习推理方法面临两大困难：结果奖励只能进行序列级优化，难以实现精确的信用分配；外部过程监督成本高昂且难以可持续扩展。因此需要一种无需外部过程监督、能从结果监督中自动提取过程级学习信号的方法。

Method: 提出监督内化（supervision-internalization）方法，从"将结果监督内化为过程监督"的新视角出发，让模型在强化学习过程中自动从失败的推理轨迹中识别错误、纠正并复用这些轨迹，从而提取过程级信用分配信号，实现细粒度的策略优化。

Result: 将监督内化思想抽象为一种新的训练范式，模型在强化学习过程中持续生成并精炼自身的内部过程监督信号，实现了不同于外部过程监督的细粒度信用分配路径。

Conclusion: 监督内化方法为在仅有结果监督的条件下实现细粒度信用分配提供了可行且可扩展的新路径，解决了强化学习推理中核心的信用分配难题。

Abstract: The central challenge of reinforcement learning for reasoning lies not only in the sparsity of outcome-level supervision, but more fundamentally in how to transform feedback provided only at the end of a sequence into fine-grained learning signals that can guide intermediate reasoning steps. Existing approaches either rely on outcome-level rewards for sequence-level optimization, which makes precise credit assignment difficult, or depend on externally constructed process supervision, which is costly and difficult to scale sustainably. To address this, we propose a new perspective: reinforcement learning for reasoning can be understood as the problem of internalizing outcome supervision into process supervision. From this perspective, we introduce a supervision-internalization method for reinforcement learning for reasoning, enabling the model to automatically extract process-level learning signals through identifying, correcting, and reusing failed reasoning trajectories, thereby achieving finer-grained policy optimization under outcome-only supervision. We further abstract this idea into a new training paradigm, in which the model continually generates and refines its own internal process supervision during reinforcement learning, opening a new path for fine-grained credit assignment in reinforcement learning for reasoning that differs from externally provided process supervision.

</details>


### [168] [Rethinking Data Curation in LLM Training: Online Reweighting Offers Better Generalization than Offline Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05227)
*Wanru Zhao,Yihong Chen,Yuzhi Tang,Wentao Ma,Shengchao Hu,Shell Xu Hu,Alex Iacob,Abhinav Mehrotra,Nicholas D. Lane*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 ADAPT，一种在线数据重加权框架，通过基于相似性质量信号的自适应逐样本学习率动态调整训练样本权重，替代传统的离线数据筛选/混合方法，在指令微调和大规模预训练中均取得更优的跨基准泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据筛选和混合方法采用离线范式，与训练过程分离，导致：(1) 模型/任务迁移时需要重新运行整个流程，引入工程开销；(2) 通过硬筛选或重采样改变数据规模，牺牲数据多样性，损害泛化能力。因此需要一种与训练过程耦合的在线数据策展方法。

Method: 提出 ADAPT (Adaptive Data reweighting for Pretraining and FineTuning)，一个动态在线框架。核心思想是将数据策展重新定义为在线重加权问题，通过基于相似性质量信号的自适应逐样本学习率，在训练过程中动态调整样本重要性（而非改变样本数量），实现隐式课程学习：随模型进化，从粗粒度模式逐步过渡到细粒度语义区分。

Result: 在指令微调和大规模预训练两个场景下，ADAPT 在同等 FLOPs 条件下，一致优于离线数据筛选/混合方法以及先前的在线方法，展现出更强的跨基准泛化能力。

Conclusion: 在线动态重加权是比离线静态数据策展更有效的数据管理范式，ADAPT 通过自适应逐样本学习率实现了无需改变数据规模的隐式课程学习，提升了训练效率和模型泛化能力。

Abstract: Data curation is a critical yet under-explored area in large language model (LLM) training. Existing methods, such as data selection and mixing, operate in an offline paradigm, detaching themselves from training. This separation introduces engineering overhead and makes the curation brittle: the entire pipeline must be re-run under model/task shifts. Moreover, offline methods alter data size through hard filtering or resampling, often sacrificing data diversity and harming generalization. We propose to rethink data curation as an online reweighting problem, where sample importance is dynamically adjusted during training via loss weighting rather than static pre-processing. Specifically, we introduce ADAPT (Adaptive Data reweighting for Pretraining and FineTuning), a dynamic online framework that reweights training samples with adaptive per-sample learning rates guided by similarity-based quality signals, without changing the number of training samples. Unlike offline methods that enforce a static data distribution, ADAPT acts as an implicit curriculum learner, progressively shifting focus from coarse-grained patterns to fine-grained semantic distinctions as the model evolves. Experiments on both instruction tuning and large-scale pretraining show that ADAPT consistently outperforms offline selection/mixing and prior online methods, achieving stronger cross-benchmark generalization under equal FLOPs.

</details>


### [169] [Attribution-Guided Continual Learning for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05285)
*Yazheng Liu,Yuxuan Wan,Rui Xu,Xi Zhang,Sihong Xie,Hui Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于归因引导的持续微调框架，通过估计Transformer层中每个参数对特定任务的重要性分数来调节梯度更新，有效缓解大语言模型在持续学习中的灾难性遗忘问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有缓解灾难性遗忘的方法（如数据回放、参数冻结、正则化）缺乏对LLM内部知识分布的语义感知，无法区分哪些参数应当保留、哪些应当更新。

Method: 提出归因引导的持续微调框架，估计每个Transformer层中参数对任务的逐元素重要性分数，用这些分数调节梯度——对先前任务重要的参数给予较小更新，不重要的参数保持可塑性以学习新任务。

Result: 在持续学习基准测试中，该方法一致性地优于基线方法，在保留旧任务知识的同时，对新任务保持有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: 通过参数级别的归因重要性引导梯度更新，能够有效平衡旧任务保留与新任务学习，为LLM持续学习提供了一种无需数据回放的高效方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) often suffer from catastrophic forgetting in continual learning: after learning new tasks sequentially, they perform worse on earlier tasks. Existing methods mitigate catastrophic forgetting by data replay, parameter freezing, or regularization. However, these methods lack semantic awareness of internal knowledge distribution in LLMs. As a result, they cannot distinguish parameters that should be preserved or updated. We propose an attribution-guided continual fine-tuning framework for LLMs. Our method estimates task-specific, element-wise parameter importance in each Transformer layer and uses these scores to modulate gradients. Parameters important to previous tasks receive smaller updates, while less relevant ones remain plastic for learning new tasks. Experiments on continual learning benchmarks show that our method consistently outperforms baselines, achieving better retention of old tasks while maintaining competitive performance on new tasks.

</details>


### [170] [COPYCOP: Ownership Verification for Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05360)
*Rahul Nandakumar,Deepayan Chakrabarti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CopyCop是一种能够识别复制模仿GNN的算法，即使对手对输出嵌入进行了变换，也能准确检测出两个GNN是否独立训练。实验表明该算法在14个数据集和5种GNN架构上准确且鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的水印和指纹识别方法无法在对手对输出嵌入进行变换、两个GNN架构/权重/嵌入维度不同的严格条件下，检测出复制模仿的GNN模型。

Method: 提出名为CopyCop的算法，能够识别复制模仿GNN，并提供了理论保证。

Result: 在14个数据集和5种GNN架构上的实验表明，CopyCop准确且鲁棒，能抵抗多种对抗攻击和变换。

Conclusion: CopyCop能够在严格条件下有效识别复制模仿GNN，优于现有方法，并具有理论保障。

Abstract: Given two GNNs that output node embeddings, how can we determine if they were trained independently? An adversary could have trained one GNN specifically to mimic the other GNN's embeddings. To obscure this relationship between the GNNs, the adversarial GNN might then transform its output embeddings. The two GNNs could have different architectures, weights, and embedding dimensions, and the adversary can transform the embeddings. Despite these stringent conditions, our algorithm (named CopyCop) can identify such copycat GNNs, unlike existing watermarking and fingerprinting methods. We also provide theoretical guarantees for CopyCop. Finally, experiments on 14 datasets and 5 GNN architectures demonstrate that CopyCop is accurate and robust against a broad class of adversarial attacks and transformations. Code is available at: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/CopyCop-Graph-Ownership-Verification-8143/README.md

</details>


### [171] [SPADE: Faster Drug Discovery by Learning from Sparse Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05370)
*Rahul Nandakumar,Ben Fauber,Deepayan Chakrabarti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SPADE是一种新型配体选择算法，仅需平均40次测试即可找到10个高质量配体，在样本效率上比深度学习和贝叶斯优化方法中位数提升7%-32%，且速度比最接近的竞争对手快10倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 药物发现中候选配体通过率极低（<5%），且需要为没有先验数据的新靶点蛋白开发高效方法，以尽可能少的测试迭代找到足够数量的高质量配体。

Method: 提出SPADE算法，采用新颖的配体选择策略，从零开始迭代选择并测试候选配体。

Result: 平均仅需40次测试即可找到10个高质量配体；在逐对比较中，SPADE在更多蛋白上优于深度学习和贝叶斯优化方法，样本效率中位数提升7%-32%；评分速度比最接近的竞争对手快10倍。

Conclusion: SPADE在样本效率和计算速度上均显著优于现有方法，为缺乏先验数据的新靶点蛋白的药物发现提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: Drug discovery seeks molecules (ligands) that bind strongly and selectively to a target protein. However, fewer than 5% of candidate ligands pass the bar for even the early stages of drug discovery. Furthermore, we want methods that work for novel proteins for which we have no prior data. Starting from scratch, we have to iteratively select and test candidate ligands such that we find enough ligands of the desired quality in as few tests as possible. Our proposed algorithm, named SPADE, introduces a novel approach to ligand selection that requires only 40 tests on average to find 10 high-quality ligands. In one-vs-one comparisons, SPADE outperforms deep learning and Bayesian optimization methods on more proteins, achieving median improvements of 7%-32% in sample efficiency. SPADE is also 10x faster than its closest competitor at scoring candidate drugs. Dataset and code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/SPADE_Fast_Drug_Discovery_by_Learning_from_Sparse_Data-F028/README.md

</details>


### [172] [Conditional Diffusion Under Linear Constraints: Langevin Mixing and Information-Theoretic Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05387)
*Ahmad Aghapour,Erhan Bayraktar,Asaf Cohen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究扩散模型用于线性逆问题的零样本条件采样，分析了投影基方法的偏差问题，提出基于法-切分解的理论分析，并设计了投影Langevin初始化与引导反向去噪方法，在图像修复和超分辨率任务上优于强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有投影基条件采样方法仅强制测量一致性，但未正确分配可行集上的概率质量，导致条件样本存在偏差。作者希望从理论上分析偏差根源并提出无偏采样方法。

Method: 引入分数函数的法-切分解：观测方向分数由测量精确确定，切线方向条件分数未知。证明用无条件切线分数替代的误差上界为观测与未观测分量间的条件互信息（维度无关），从而将误差分解为初始化误差和路径分数失配误差。据此提出投影Langevin初始化 + 引导反向去噪。

Result: 在图像修复和超分辨率实验中，所提方法在定量指标上优于强投影基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过信息论分解阐明了扩散模型条件采样偏差的来源，投影Langevin初始化结合引导反向去噪可有效减小误差，实现更准确的条件生成。

Abstract: We study zero-shot conditional sampling with pretrained diffusion models for linear inverse problems, including inpainting and super-resolution. In these problems, the observation determines only part of the unknown signal. The remaining degrees of freedom must be sampled according to the correct conditional data distribution. Existing projection-based samplers enforce measurement consistency by correcting the observed component during reverse diffusion. However, measurement consistency alone does not determine how probability mass should be distributed along the feasible set, and this can lead to biased conditional samples.
  We analyze this issue through a normal--tangent decomposition of the score function. For Gaussian noising, the observed-direction score is exactly determined by the measurement; only the tangent conditional score is unknown. We prove that the error from replacing this score by the unconditional tangent score is upper bounded by a dimension-free conditional mutual information between observed and unobserved components. This gives an information-theoretic decomposition into initialization and pathwise score-mismatch errors. Motivated by the theory, we propose a projected-Langevin initialization followed by guided reverse denoising, which outperforms a strong projection-based baseline in inpainting and super-resolution experiments.

</details>


### [173] [Two-Stage Learned Decomposition for Scalable Routing on Multigraphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05389)
*Filip Rydin,Morteza Haghir Chehreghani,Balázs Kulcsár*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出节点-边策略分解（NEPF）方法，通过将路由策略分解为节点排列阶段和边选择阶段，解决多图车辆路径问题中的可扩展性问题，在六个VRP变体上达到或超越SOTA性能，同时显著提升训练和推理速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经方法多局限于欧几里得设置或简单图，而多图场景（平行边代表不同旅行选项）缺乏高效方法，且已有方法存在严重的可扩展性问题。

Method: 提出NEPF方法，将路由策略分解为节点排列阶段和边选择阶段；引入预编码边聚合方案和非自回归架构用于边阶段；采用分层强化学习联合训练两个阶段。

Result: 在六个VRP变体上的实验表明，NEPF在解质量上与SOTA相当或更优，同时在训练和推理速度上显著更快。

Conclusion: NEPF有效缓解了多图VRP的可扩展性问题，为更复杂的现实路由场景提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: Most neural methods for Vehicle Routing Problems (VRPs) are limited to Euclidean settings or simple graphs. In this work, we instead consider multigraphs, where parallel edges represent distinct travel options with varying trade-offs (e.g., distance vs time). Few methods are designed for such formulations and those that do exist face major scalability issues. We mitigate these scalability issues via a Node-Edge Policy Factorization (NEPF) approach, which splits the routing policy into a node permutation stage and an edge selection stage. To enable the decomposition, we introduce a pre-encoding edge aggregation scheme and a non-autoregressive architecture for the edge stage, as well as a hierarchical reinforcement learning method to train the stages jointly. Our experiments across six VRP variants demonstrate that NEPF matches or outperforms the state-of-the-art in terms of solution quality, while being significantly faster in training and inference.

</details>


### [174] [Differentiable Parameter Optimization for DAEs with State-Dependent Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05395)
*Ion Matei,Maksym Zhenirovskyy,Anthony Wong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了含事件驱动的半显式微分代数方程（DAE）系统的可微参数优化，提出了两种互补的梯度计算方法：一种是基于自动微分通过模拟的方法，另一种是显式离散伴随方法。两种方法均在固定事件顺序和横向约束穿越条件下有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 含状态事件驱动的DAE系统在工程中广泛存在，但由于代数变量隐式定义、事件时间依赖参数、重置映射引入不连续性，其基于梯度的参数学习面临严峻挑战，现有方法难以有效处理这些困难。

Method: 将学习问题建模为带DAE动力学、代数约束、守卫方程和重置映射的约束最小二乘问题。提出两种梯度计算策略：(1) 自动微分通过模拟方法，在向量场中求解代数变量，利用隐函数定理微分代数求解，通过分段可微积分处理事件；(2) 显式离散伴随方法，将前向模拟表示为事件分裂残差系统，通过求解光滑段和事件残差的拉格朗日乘子计算梯度。

Result: 两种方法均能为前向模拟选定的事件路径提供梯度，并在固定事件顺序和横向守卫穿越条件下有效。论文从梯度解释、事件时间处理、实现复杂度和局部有效性四个维度对两种方法进行了比较。

Conclusion: 本文明确了伴随方法中的残差项是等式约束而非启发式惩罚项，为含事件DAE系统的可微参数优化提供了理论基础和实用方法。

Abstract: Differential-algebraic equations (DAEs) with state-dependent events arise in systems whose continuous dynamics are constrained by algebraic equations and interrupted by mode changes, switching logic, impacts, or state reinitializations. Gradient-based parameter learning for such systems is challenging because algebraic variables are implicitly defined, event times depend on the parameters, and reset maps introduce discontinuities. This paper studies differentiable parameter optimization for semi-explicit DAEs with events. We formulate the learning problem as a constrained least-squares problem with DAE dynamics, algebraic constraints, guard equations, and reset maps. We then develop two complementary gradient-computation strategies. The first is an automatic-differentiation-through-simulation method that solves algebraic variables inside the vector field, differentiates the algebraic solve using the implicit function theorem, and handles events through segmented differentiable integration. The second is an explicit discrete-adjoint method that represents the forward simulation as an event-split residual system and computes gradients by solving for the Lagrange multipliers of smooth-segment and event residuals. The formulation clarifies that residual terms in the adjoint method are equality constraints, not heuristic penalties. We compare the two approaches in terms of gradient interpretation, event-time handling, implementation complexity, and local validity. Both methods provide gradients for the event path selected by the forward simulation and are valid under fixed event ordering and transversal guard crossings.

</details>


### [175] [Distributionally Robust Multi-Objective Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05660)
*Yufeng Yang,Fangning Zhuo,Ziyi Chen,Heng Huang,Yi Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出分布鲁棒多目标优化（DR-MOO）框架，应对数据分布偏移下的多目标优化问题，设计了双循环和单循环两种多梯度下降算法，分别达到O(ε^{-12})和O(ε^{-4})的样本复杂度，实验表明与现有方法竞争力强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MOO方法未考虑数据分布偏移（分布鲁棒性），实际应用中数据分布常发生变化，需要一种同时处理多个目标及其各自最坏分布偏移的优化框架。

Method: (1) 提出DR-MOO的Pareto型解概念；(2) 利用拉格朗日对偶重构，设计双环MGDA（内环估计对偶变量），样本复杂度O(ε^{-12})；(3) 引入梯度裁剪处理广义光滑和有偏梯度估计，提出单环双裁剪MGDA，样本复杂度提升至O(ε^{-4})。

Result: 理论：双环算法O(ε^{-12})，单环双裁剪算法O(ε^{-4})，适用于非凸设定且无需有界目标或梯度假设。实验：与现有SOTA MGDA基线方法竞争性相当。

Conclusion: DR-MOO有效填补了MOO与分布鲁棒性之间的空白，单环算法在理论效率上有显著提升，为实际应用中处理多目标分布偏移提供了可行的优化工具。

Abstract: Multi-objective optimization (MOO) has received growing attention in applications that require learning under multiple criteria. However, the existing MOO formulations do not explicitly account for distributional shifts in the data. We introduce distributionally robust multi-objective optimization (DR-MOO), which minimizes multiple objectives under their respective worst-case distributions. We propose Pareto-type solution concepts for DR-MOO and develop multi-gradient descent algorithms (MGDA) with provable guarantees. Leveraging a Lagrangian dual reformulation, we first design a double-loop MGDA that uses an inner loop to estimate dual variables and achieves a total sample complexity $\mathcal{O}(ε^{-12})$ for reaching an $ε$-Pareto-stationary point. To further improve efficiency, we incorporate gradient clipping to handle generalized-smooth and biased gradient estimates, removing the need for double sampling. This yields a single-loop double-clip MGDA with substantially improved sample complexity $\mathcal{O}(ε^{-4})$. Our theory applies to the nonconvex setting and does not require bounded objectives or gradients. Experiments demonstrate that our methods are competitive with state-of-the-art MGDA baselines.

</details>


### [176] [Information Theoretic Adversarial Training of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05415)
*Yiwei Zhang,Jeremiah Birrell,Reza Ebrahimi,Rouzbeh Behnia,Jason Pacheco,Elisa Bertino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: WARDEN是一种基于分布鲁棒优化的对抗训练框架，通过f-散度模糊集动态重加权对抗样本，在KL散度下将目标简化为log-sum-exp形式，显著降低LLM攻击成功率，同时保持与现有方法相当的计算效率和模型实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM对抗训练方法计算成本高、难以扩展，而连续对抗训练方法（如CAT、CAPO）虽更高效，但仍需进一步提升鲁棒性。

Method: 提出WARDEN框架，通过f-散度（KL散度）模糊集围绕经验训练分布构建分布鲁棒优化目标，动态重加权对抗样本以强调更难样本；利用凸对偶形式将目标简化为log-sum-exp形式，并由动态参数控制重加权强度。

Result: 在多个LLM和攻击设置下，WARDEN显著降低攻击成功率，计算和效用成本与CAT、CAPO、MixAT等方法相当。

Conclusion: WARDEN提供了一种实用且可扩展的鲁棒对齐方法，通过信息论目标在保持模型性能的同时有效提升对抗鲁棒性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) remain vulnerable to adversarial prompting despite advances in alignment and safety, often exhibiting harmful behaviors under novel attack strategies. While adversarial training can improve robustness, existing approaches are computationally expensive and difficult to scale. Recent continuous adversarial training methods, such as Continuous adversarial training (CAT) and Continuous Adversarial Preference Optimization (CAPO), address this challenge by leveraging gradient-based perturbations in the embedding space, enabling more efficient and expressive attacks. Building on this paradigm, we propose WARDEN, a distributionally robust adversarial training framework for LLMs that dynamically reweights adversarial examples through an f -divergence ambiguity set around the empirical training distribution. Our method optimizes the worst-case adversarial loss within a divergence ball around the empirical data distribution, automatically emphasizing harder adversarial examples. Using the convex dual formulation, the objective reduces to a log-sum-exp form under the KL divergence, with a dynamical parameter controlling the strength of reweighting. This study leads to a new class of information-theoretic objectives that significantly reduce attack success rates while maintaining model utility. Across multiple LLMs and attack settings, WARDEN substantially reduces attack success rates with computational and utility costs comparable to CAT-, CAPO-, and MixAT-based baselines, making it a practical approach for scalable robust alignment.

</details>


### [177] [Active Learning for Conditional Generative Compressed Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05435)
*Alexander DeLise,Nick Dexter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于提示条件生成模型的压缩感知问题，将提示在采样分布设计和恢复模型中区分开来，理论证明了提示匹配的Christoffel采样保持近最优的生成压缩感知复杂度常数，而提示不匹配会带来兼容性惩罚。实验表明提示能有效重塑Christoffel采样分布并影响图像恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索提示条件在生成压缩感知中的双重作用（采样分布设计与恢复模型定义），并量化提示匹配与不匹配对恢复性能的理论影响。

Method: 针对ReLU和Lipschitz条件生成器，推导稳定恢复界；通过Christoffel采样理论分析提示匹配/不匹配下的复杂度常数；使用Stable Diffusion进行实验验证。

Result: 提示匹配的Christoffel采样保持与近最优生成压缩感知理论相同的Christoffel复杂度常数；提示不匹配引入显式兼容性惩罚；实验证明提示能重塑采样分布并影响恢复质量。

Conclusion: 提示应被视为设计变量，在感知、近似和恢复中具有不同效应，为基于提示的条件生成压缩感知提供了理论保障。

Abstract: Generative compressed sensing uses the range of a pretrained generator as a nonlinear model for recovering structured signals from limited measurements. We study a conditional version of this problem for image recovery from subsampled Fourier measurements using prompt-conditioned generative models. Our framework separates two roles of conditioning: the prompt used to design the sampling distribution and the prompt used to define the recovery model. For ReLU and Lipschitz conditional generators, we prove stable recovery bounds showing that prompt-matched Christoffel sampling retains the same Christoffel complexity constant as existing near-optimal generative compressed sensing theory, while prompt mismatch incurs an explicit compatibility penalty. Experiments with Stable Diffusion show that prompts meaningfully reshape Christoffel sampling distributions and influence image recovery. Overall, our results suggest that prompts should be treated as design variables with distinct effects on sensing, approximation, and recovery.

</details>


### [178] [Quadratic Objective Perturbation: Curvature-Based Differential Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05905)
*Daniel Cortild,Coralia Cartis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出二次目标扰动（QOP）方法，用随机二次型扰动目标函数，通过曲率而非梯度有界假设实现差分隐私，在插值区域中放宽了传统线性目标扰动（LOP）对梯度有界的要求，并提供了隐私保证、效用分析及高效求解算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性目标扰动（LOP）需要损失函数梯度有界的强假设，这排除了许多现代机器学习模型。本文旨在消除这一限制，在更弱的假设下实现差分隐私经验风险最小化。

Method: 提出二次目标扰动（QOP），用随机二次型扰动目标函数。通过曲率而非梯度有界性来诱导强凸性和稳定性，利用扰动的谱性质控制灵敏度，从而在插值区域中实现(ε, δ)-差分隐私。同时分析了近似解下的隐私保持问题，并采用现代分裂格式高效求解。

Result: 在插值区域中，QOP在更弱的假设下实现了(ε, δ)-差分隐私；近似解下的隐私保证依然成立；给出了经验超额风险的效用界；理论分析与数值实验表明曲率基扰动相比LOP具有优势。

Conclusion: QOP通过曲率控制而非梯度有界假设实现了差分隐私，放宽了LOP的限制，适用于更广泛的模型，且可高效求解，具有较强的理论保障和实用价值。

Abstract: Objective perturbation is a standard mechanism in differentially private empirical risk minimization. In particular, Linear Objective Perturbation (LOP) enforces privacy by adding a random linear term, while strong convexity and stability are ensured by an additional deterministic quadratic term. However, this approach requires the strong assumption of bounded gradients of the loss function, which excludes many modern machine learning models. In this work, we introduce Quadratic Objective Perturbation (QOP), which perturbs the objective with a random quadratic form. This perturbation induces strong convexity and enforces stability of the problem through curvature, thereby enabling privacy and allowing sensitivity to be controlled through spectral properties of the perturbation rather than assumptions on the gradients. As a result, we obtain $(\varepsilon, δ)$-differential privacy under weaker assumptions, in the interpolation regime. Furthermore, we extend the analysis to account for approximate solutions, showing that privacy guarantees are preserved under inexact solves. Additionally, we derive utility guarantees in terms of empirical excess risk, and provide a theoretical and numerical comparison to LOP, highlighting the advantages of curvature-based perturbations. Finally, we discuss algorithmic aspects and show that the resulting problems can be solved efficiently using modern splitting schemes.

</details>


### [179] [On Semantic Loss Fine-Tuning Approach for Preventing Model Collapse in Causal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05438)
*Pratik Deshmukh,Atirek Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 标准微调Transformer模型会导致因果推理任务中的灾难性模型崩溃（总是预测"是"或"否"）。本文提出一种带有图基逻辑约束和动态lambda调度的语义损失函数，在Gemma 270M上实现70.4%（传递性任务）和68.6%（d-分割任务）的准确率，相比崩溃基线提升42.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准微调Transformer模型进行因果推理时，模型会崩溃为学习琐碎的解（如总是预测"是"或"否"），无法学习真正的因果推理能力。

Method: 提出语义损失函数，包含基于图的逻辑约束和动态lambda调度，防止模型崩溃，实现稳定的上下文相关预测。

Result: 传递性任务准确率70.4%，d-分割任务准确率68.6%，相比崩溃基线提升42.7%；在20万+评估样本上验证有效性。

Conclusion: 语义损失对于Transformer实现稳定的因果推理是必要的，而非可选的附加组件。

Abstract: Standard fine-tuning of transformer models on causal reasoning tasks leads to catastrophic model collapse, where models learn trivial solutions such as always predicting "Yes" or "No" regardless of input structure. We demonstrate that fine-tuning Gemma 270M on transitivity and d-separation tasks without semantic loss results in 100% collapse rate, with models achieving misleadingly high accuracy (73.9%) while learning no causal reasoning. We propose a semantic loss function with graph-based logical constraints and dynamic lambda scheduling that prevents this collapse. Our approach achieves 70.4% accuracy on transitivity tasks and 68.6% on d-separation tasks with stable, context-dependent predictions, representing a 42.7% improvement over collapsed baselines. Adversarial evaluation on 1,000 structural reasoning samples shows semantic models achieve 67-70% accuracy while collapsed models fail catastrophically at 43-71%. We validate our findings through comprehensive benchmarking on 200,000+ evaluation samples across five model variants, demonstrating that semantic loss is essential and not optional, for stable causal reasoning in transformers.

</details>


### [180] [Robustness of Graph Self-Supervised Learning to Real-World Noise: A Case Study on Text-Driven Biomedical Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05463)
*Othmane Kabal,Mounira Harzallah,Fabrice Guillet,Hideaki Takeda,Ryutaro Ichise*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统评估了图自监督学习在文本驱动噪声图上的表现，提出了NATD-GSSL框架，发现关系重构对噪声敏感、特征重构鲁棒性强、对比学习受噪声影响较小但依赖下游任务对齐，双向关系消息传递架构更适应噪声图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GSSL方法假设图是干净的、人工精心整理的，而NLP技术自动提取的知识图谱存在大量真实噪声，已有鲁棒性研究仅关注合成扰动，未探索此类真实噪声的影响。

Method: 提出NATD-GSSL统一框架，包含自动图构建、图细化和GSSL三个模块；采用双图评估协议，将MedMentions导出的噪声图与干净的UMLS参考图进行对比，通过共享金标准对齐。

Result: 关系重构对噪声高度敏感，但受益于良好定义的Schema；特征重构鲁棒性最强，性能接近干净图设置；对比学习受噪声影响较小，但严重依赖与下游任务的对齐；双向关系消息传递GNN更适合噪声图，单向关系GNN在干净图上表现最佳；相比预训练语言模型基线提升高达7%。

Conclusion: 为GSSL在真实噪声图上的应用提供了实用指导，不同预训练目标和GNN架构对噪声的鲁棒性存在显著差异，需根据噪声特性选择合适的组件。

Abstract: Graph Self-Supervised Learning (GSSL) offers a powerful paradigm for learning graph representations without labeled data. However, existing work assumes clean, manually curated graphs. Recent advances in NLP enable the large-scale automatic extraction of knowledge graphs from text, opening new opportunities for GSSL while introducing substantial real-world noise. This type of noise remains largely unexplored, as prior robustness studies typically rely on synthetic perturbations. To address this gap, we present the first comprehensive evaluation of GSSL methods on text-driven graphs for unsupervised term typing. We introduce Noise-Aware Text-Driven Graph GSSL (NATD-GSSL), a unified framework that combines automatic graph construction, graph refinement, and GSSL. Our evaluation follows a dual-graph protocol that contrasts a noisy graph derived from MedMentions with a clean Unified Medical Language System (UMLS) reference graph, aligned through a shared gold standard. Our results reveal variability in robustness across both pretext tasks and Graph Neural Network (GNN) architectures. Relation reconstruction is highly sensitive to noise and benefits from well-defined schemas, whereas feature reconstruction is considerably more robust, achieving performance comparable to clean-graph settings. Contrastive objectives are generally less affected by noise but depend strongly on alignment with downstream tasks. GNN architecture also plays a critical role: bidirectional relational message-passing designs are better suited to noisy, text-driven graphs, while unidirectional relational ones perform best on clean graphs. Overall, NATD-GSSL provides practical guidance for applying GSSL to real-world, noisy graphs and achieves up to a 7\% improvement over pretrained language model baselines. All code and benchmarks are publicly available at https://github.com/OthmaneKabal/MC2GAE.

</details>


### [181] [Sharper Guarantees for Misspecified Kernelized Bandit Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05967)
