For unsolved instances within a 10-minute limit, optimality gaps are under 13%, highlighting the formulation's tightness. We further benchmark our approach against three proposed baseline formulations and another algorithm from a related problem, and demonstrate that our formulation outperforms all baselines.

</details>


### [328] [Optimal Design of Stealthy Attacks in Partially Observed Linear Systems: A Likelihood-Based Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05545)
*Haosheng Zhou,Ruimeng Hu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了针对部分观测线性控制系统的隐蔽攻击最优设计问题，提出了基于新息过程的似然检测机制，量化了攻击的隐蔽性与性能损失之间的权衡，并在确定性和自适应两种信息结构下开发了可处理的最优攻击框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有隐蔽攻击设计缺乏统一的理论框架，尤其是在攻击者仅能部分观测系统状态时，如何平衡攻击效能与可检测性仍未得到充分解决。

Method: 提出基于新息过程的似然检测机制量化隐蔽性；针对确定性攻击和自适应攻击分别建模；对于自适应攻击下的部分观测问题，采用分层优化框架结合分离原理，转化为马尔可夫控制问题并得到半解析最优攻击。

Result: 建立了最优隐蔽攻击的可处理控制理论框架；证明了系统的适定性；通过数值实验展示了信息约束如何影响攻击效能与隐蔽性之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 信息结构（确定性vs自适应）显著影响隐蔽攻击的最优设计，所提出的框架为分析部分观测场景下攻击的隐蔽性与效能权衡提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We study the optimal design of stealthy attacks against partially observed linear control systems. We first propose a novel likelihood-based detection mechanism derived from the innovation process, based on which we quantify stealthiness and formulate an attack design problem that trades off performance degradation and detectability. We develop a tractable control-theoretic framework for optimal stealthy attacks under two information structures: deterministic attacks fixed prior to system evolution, and adaptive attacks constructed from available observations. In the adaptive setting, the attacker's partial observation leads to a stochastic control problem with an endogenous information structure. We address this challenge through a hierarchical optimization framework combined with the separation principle, reducing the problem to a Markovian control formulation and yielding semi-explicit optimal attacks. We further establish well-posedness of the resulting systems and illustrate through numerical experiments how information constraints shape the trade-off between attack effectiveness and stealthiness.

</details>


### [329] [Stability of the Monge Map in Semi-Dual Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05569)
*Anton Selitskiy,David Millard*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文揭示了最优传输半对偶公式的退化鞍点结构，证明其数值解等价于求解约束优化问题，并推导了Monge映射收敛的充要条件，解释了实际算法中传输映射更新比势函数需要更多迭代的原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最优传输问题的半对偶公式虽然广泛应用，但其数值求解中传输映射与势函数的更新速度存在显著差异（映射需要更多迭代），这一现象缺乏理论解释。本文旨在从理论上揭示这一现象的根本原因。

Method: 1. 分析半对偶最优传输问题的鞍点结构，证明其具有退化性；2. 将数值求解等价转化为约束优化问题；3. 在不要求对偶势达到最优的条件下，推导Monge映射收敛的充要条件。

Result: 1. 证明半对偶最优传输问题具有退化鞍点结构；2. 建立数值解与约束优化问题的等价性；3. 给出Monge映射收敛的充要条件（无需对偶势最优性）。

Conclusion: 本文从理论层面解释了为何在最优传输数值算法中，传输映射的更新通常比对偶势函数需要更多迭代次数，为改进算法效率提供了理论指导。

Abstract: This paper shows that the semi-dual formulation of the optimal transport problem has a degenerate saddle-point structure, and that its numerical solution is equivalent to solving a constrained optimization problem. We derive necessary and sufficient conditions for the convergence of Monge maps without requiring optimality of the dual potential. This analysis helps explain why, in practice, numerical algorithms often require more iterations to update the transport map than the potential.

</details>


### [330] [A closer target setting approach to boundary problems with the Russell graph measure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05860)
*Atsushi Hori,Kazuyuki Sekitani*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对Russell图测度（RGM）模型在非负象限边界处的边界问题，提出了一种结合更接近目标设置方法与生产权衡的改进模型，该模型克服了边界问题，具有比现有模型更强的性质，且效率得分可通过求解一系列线性规划计算，实验表明其目标更现实。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Russell图测度（RGM）作为标准DEA模型之一，其效率测度在非负象限边界处存在定义不良或性质不可接受的问题（即边界问题）。现有研究虽尝试解决，但其模型可能无法识别有效目标，或无法满足效率测度的某些理想性质。

Method: 将更接近目标设置方法（closer target setting approach）融入带有生产权衡（production trade-offs）的RGM模型中，通过引入生产权衡信息，使目标点更贴近实际生产前沿；效率得分通过求解一系列线性规划（LPs）计算。

Result: 所提模型克服了边界问题，效率测度具有比现有模型更强的性质（如满足更多理想公理）；数值实验表明，基于真实数据集，所提模型提供的目标比现有模型更现实可行。

Conclusion: 本文提出的模型有效解决了RGM的边界问题，在理论和应用层面均优于现有模型，验证了模型在实际应用中的有效性。

Abstract: A Russell graph measure (RGM) is one of the standard DEA models, but its efficiency measure is not well-defined--or has unacceptable properties--at the boundary of the nonnegative orthant. This is known as a boundary problem. Existing studies have tackled this issue; however, their models may fail to identify an efficient target or fail to satisfy some desirable properties of efficiency measures. In this paper, we incorporate a closer target setting approach into the RGM model with production trade-offs to overcome such issues. We demonstrate that the efficiency measure of the proposed model overcomes the boundary problem and has stronger properties than existing models. We also demonstrate that the efficiency scores of the proposed model can be computed by solving a series of LPs. We conduct a numerical experiment with a real-world dataset to illustrate how targets provided by our model are realistic compared with the existing model, which also suggests the validity of our model in applications.

</details>


### [331] [Universal Adaptive Proximal Gradient Methods via Gradient Mapping Accumulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05944)
*Zimeng Wang,Alp Yurtsever*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种自适应近端梯度方法，用于最小化两个函数之和，其中一个是简单凸函数，另一个属于非凸光滑、凸非光滑或凸光滑三类之一。该方法的关键是自适应步长，分母累积历史梯度映射范数。无需修改或知道问题参数，方法在温和的有界迭代和有界方差假设下，对所有三类问题均收敛，收敛速率与近端梯度法匹配（至多对数因子差异），适用于确定性和随机环境。针对凸情形，进一步提出加速变体：非光滑情形保持近最优收敛率，光滑情形达到$\widetilde{O}\big(1/t^2 + σ/\sqrt{t}\big)$的改进速率（至多对数因子最优）。开发了控制随机噪声影响的新技术，适用于随机环境中的所有三类问题，并简化了分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自适应方法通常需要问题参数知识或仅针对特定问题类别设计，缺乏一个统一的、无需参数调节就能同时处理非凸光滑、凸非光滑和凸光滑三类问题的自适应近端梯度框架。

Method: 提出自适应近端梯度法，步长设计为分母累积历史梯度映射范数；在凸情形下进一步引入加速变体；开发了控制随机噪声的新分析技术。

Result: 在确定性和随机设置下，三类问题均达到与近端梯度法匹配的收敛速率（至多对数因子差异）。加速变体在凸光滑情形达到$\widetilde{O}\big(1/t^2 + σ/\sqrt{t}\big)$的最优速率。

Conclusion: 所提自适应方法统一高效地解决了三类优化问题，无需参数先验知识，理论上达到近最优收敛保证，且随机噪声控制技术简化了分析并具广泛适用性。

Abstract: We propose an adaptive proximal gradient method for minimizing the sum of two functions, where one is a simple convex function, and the other belongs to one of the three classes: nonconvex smooth, convex nonsmooth, or convex smooth. The key feature of the method is an adaptive step size that accumulates historical gradient mapping norms in the denominator. Without any modification or knowledge of problem parameters, the method converges across all three problem classes under mild bounded-iterates and bounded-variance assumptions, with rates matching those of the proximal gradient method up to logarithmic factors, in both deterministic and stochastic settings. For the convex setting, we further propose an accelerated variant. It retains a similar near-optimal convergence rate for the nonsmooth case and achieves an improved rate of order $\widetilde{O}\big(1/t^2 + σ/\sqrt{t}\big)$ for the smooth case, which is optimal up to logarithmic factors. Notably, we develop new techniques for controlling the effect of stochastic noise, which are applicable across all three problem classes in the stochastic setting and enable simplified analysis.

</details>


### [332] [Distortion-minimized de-homogenization for optimization of cell-size distribution in TPMS structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06011)
*Hiroki Kawabe,Kaito Ohtani,Yusibo Yang,Musaddiq Al Ali,Kentaro Yaji*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于三周期极小曲面（TPMS）结构单元尺寸空间优化的均匀化拓扑优化方法，通过一种新颖的去均匀化技术（基于离散余弦变换求解泊松方程）显著降低了传统周期调制方法的结构畸变，在刚度最大化问题中实现了更优的结构响应（应变能差异从63.6%降至0.8%，实验刚度提升54.2%而传统方法下降77.3%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TPMS结构的均匀化拓扑优化中，传统周期调制（PM）去均匀化方法会产生严重的结构畸变，导致优化后的结构响应与均匀化模型预测值之间存在巨大偏差（应变能差异高达63.6%），严重制约了均匀化TO方法在TPMS空间单元尺寸优化中的实际效用。

Method: 提出一种新颖的去均匀化技术，通过直接最小化目标波数与实际波数分布之间的差异来消除畸变，将该最小化问题转化为典型的泊松方程并利用离散余弦变换高效求解。将该去均匀化方法嵌入刚度最大化的均匀化拓扑优化框架中。

Result: 数值验证表明所提方法显著降低了去均匀化结构的畸变；刚度最大化问题中，所提方法的去均匀化结构与均匀化模型应变能差异仅为0.8%（PM方法为63.6%）；去均匀化模型有限元分析显示，所提方法优化结构的应变能比均匀情况提升50.1%，而PM方法下降45.8%；实验验证所提方法优化结构的有效刚度比均匀情况高54.2%，PM方法下降77.3%。

Conclusion: 所提出的去均匀化方法有效提高了TPMS结构单元尺寸优化的准确性，充分发挥了均匀化拓扑优化方法的潜力，为TPMS结构的性能最优化提供了可靠的技术路径。

Abstract: This paper presents a homogenized topology optimization (TO) method for spatially optimizing cell-size distribution of triply-periodic minimal surface (TPMS) structures, with high accuracy in the optimized structural response after de-homogenization. To achieve this, we introduce a novel de-homogenization technique that directly minimizes the difference between the wavenumbers obtained from the target and actual size distributions. This minimization problem is efficiently solved as a typical Poisson's equation utilizing the discrete cosine transform. We first verify the proposed de-homogenization method through numerical examples, showcasing its capability in significantly reducing the known distortion of the de-homogenized TPMS structures from the conventional periodic modulation (PM) method. Then, we apply the proposed method to a stiffness maximization problem, to demonstrate its effectiveness in improving the structural response compared to the PM method. The proposed method successfully reduced the distortion of the de-homogenized structures compared to the PM method, leading to 0.8% difference in the strain energy compared to the homogenized model, as opposed to 63.6% difference in the PM method. The optimized structure from the proposed method shows a significant improvement in the strain energy by 50.1% compared to the uniform case in the FE analysis on the de-homogenized models, while the PM method results in a significant decrease of 45.8%. The experimental validation shows that the effective stiffness of the optimized structure from the proposed method is 54.2% higher than that of the uniform case, while the PM method results in a significant decrease by 77.3%. These results exhibit the proposed method effectively increases the accuracy of the de-homogenization, thereby maximizing the potential of the homogenized TO for the spatial cell-size optimization of TPMS structures.

</details>


### [333] [A Unified Zeroth-Order Proximal Newton-Type Framework for Composite Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06016)
*Zekun Liu,Jinyan Fan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种统一的免导数近端牛顿型算法框架，用于求解黑箱函数与已知正则项之和的复合优化问题，并建立了非凸和强凸设置下的迭代与预言复杂度界，以及基于Dennis-Moré条件的局部R-超线性收敛性，理论上解决了BFGS方案与有限差分梯度估计器更兼容的开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有免导数优化方法在处理复合优化问题时效率有限，且关于BFGS拟牛顿法在免导数框架下与不同梯度估计器兼容性的理论分析尚不明确，存在开放问题。

Method: 提出统一的无导数近端牛顿型算法框架，结合有限差分和基于平滑的梯度估计器，建立非凸和强凸情形下的复杂度理论，并通过Dennis-Moré条件分析局部收敛速率。

Result: 建立了算法在非凸和强凸情形下的迭代与预言复杂度界；证明了基于Dennis-Moré条件的局部R-超线性收敛性；从理论上证明了BFGS与有限差分梯度估计器更兼容。

Conclusion: 提出的免导数近端牛顿型算法在理论和实验上都表现高效，且阐明了BFGS更新与不同梯度估计器的适配性，解决了该领域的开放问题。

Abstract: We propose a unified derivative-free proximal Newton-type algorithm framework for solving composite optimization problems formulated as the sum of a black-box function and a known regularization term. We establish the iteration and oracle complexity bounds for the algorithm to attain an $ε$-optimal solution under both nonconvex and strongly convex settings. We also establish its local R-superlinear convergence based on the Dennis--Moré condition, and theoretically address an open problem by showing that the BFGS scheme is more compatible with finite-difference gradient estimators than with smoothing-based ones. Numerical experiments are further presented to demonstrate the efficiency of the proposed method.

</details>


### [334] [Solving Constrained Affine Heaviside Composite Optimization Problems by a Progressive IP Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06020)
*Ke Zheng,Junyi Liu,Yurui Wang,Jong-Shi Pang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种渐进整数规划（PIP）方法，通过近似修正半连续性和闭性缺陷，结合逐次分解策略，有效求解含Heaviside复合函数的不连续优化问题，并在多类分类问题上验证了收敛性和有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Heaviside复合优化问题（A-HSCOPs）中，Heaviside函数的不连续性导致目标函数缺乏半连续性和可行集不封闭，且内部函数不可微，传统优化方法难以直接求解。

Method: 首先通过近似方法修正目标函数半连续性和约束闭性缺陷，随后采用渐进整数规划（PIP）方法结合逐次分解，处理Heaviside函数固有的离散性质。

Result: 建立了算法收敛到局部最优解的理论保证，并在基于分数和基于树的多类分类问题（含精度约束）上通过大量计算实验验证了整体求解策略的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的近似+PIP方法能够有效克服Heaviside复合优化中的不连续、非封闭和不可微等挑战，为这类特殊非光滑优化问题提供了可行的求解框架。

Abstract: This paper discusses the computational resolution and presents numerical results for solving affine combinations of Heaviside composite optimization problems (abbreviated as A-HSCOPs) by a progressive integer programming (abbreviated as PIP) method. The characteristics of these problems are that the Heaviside functions, which appear in the objective and define the constraints, are discontinuous, and their mixed-signed combinations result in the overall objective lacking the matching semicontinuity needed for the optimization and in the feasible set being not necessarily closed. Added to these challenging properties is the nondifferentiability of the inner functions in the composition. In this paper, we propose resolutions to all these challenges by first an approximation to remedy the lack of semicontinuity in the objective and closedness in the constraints, followed by a progressive integer programming approach with successive decomposition to handle the intrinsically discrete nature of the Heaviside function. Convergence to the local optimizers of the given Heaviside optimization problem is established. The effectiveness of the overall solution strategy is supported by extensive computational experiments on the score-based and tree-based multiclass classification problems with precision constraints.

</details>


### [335] [Convergence guarantees for stochastic algorithms solving non-unique problems in metric spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06129)
*Nicholas Pischke,Thomas Powell*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文在广义度量空间（CAT(0)空间）中，对随机拟Fejér单调序列建立了定量收敛定理，显式构造了均值和几乎必然意义下的收敛速率，该结果统一并推广了多种常见随机优化与不动点算法的收敛性分析，并通过随机近端点法、随机Krasnoselskii-Mann迭代和Busemann次梯度法三个实例验证了其应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Kohlenbach等人关于确定性拟Fejér单调序列的定量收敛分析推广到随机设定，并建立统一的度量框架，使得多种常见的随机算法（如随机近端点法、随机不动点迭代等）的收敛速率分析能够在同一理论下得到处理。

Method: 提出随机拟Fejér单调序列的概念，结合随机正则性假设（从确定性正则性概念推广而来），显式构造均值和几乎必然收敛的速率；该正则性假设统一了广义压缩性、弱尖锐极小值、误差界、一致单调性、全局度量次正则性、Polyak-Łojasiewicz条件以及Kurdyka-Łojasiewicz条件等。

Result: 给出了随机拟Fejér单调序列在均值和几乎必然意义下的显式收敛速率；在特殊情况下可构造线性非渐近保证；并在三个具体方法（随机近端点法、随机Krasnoselskii-Mann方案、Busemann次梯度法）上得到了新的收敛速率。

Conclusion: 本文建立的统一定量框架能够广泛应用于非正曲率完备测地度量空间（CAT(0)空间）上的随机近似算法，为这些方法的收敛性分析提供了系统且高度统一的工具。

Abstract: We prove a general quantitative theorem on the asymptotic behavior of stochastic quasi-Fejér monotone sequences in a broad metric context. Concretely, our result explicitly constructs a rate of convergence for such process, both in mean and almost surely, under an abstract stochastic regularity assumption, derived from previous work of Kohlenbach, López-Acedo and Nicolae [Isr. J. Math. 232(1), pp. 261-297, 2019] on such notions in a deterministic context. Our notion of regularity extends and unifies many common conditions from the literature, such as generalized contractivity for self maps, weak sharp minima and error bounds for real-valued functions, uniform monotonicity and global metric subregularity for set-valued operators, related Polyak-Łojasiewicz or Kurdyka-Łojasiewicz conditions, as well as expected sharp growth as e.g. studied by Asi and Duchi [SIAM J. Optim. 29(3), pp. 2257-2290, 2019]. The rate is moreover highly uniform, depending only on very few data of the surrounding objects. We also discuss special cases which allow for the construction of fast rates in the form of linear non-asymptotic guarantees. We conclude by presenting three concrete methods from stochastic approximation where our results yield new rates of convergence, including the classical example of the stochastic proximal point method, a randomized variant of the Krasnoselskii-Mann scheme for solving stochastic fixed-point equations, and a Busemann subgradient method recently introduced by Goodwin, Lewis, López-Acedo and Nicolae [Math. Program., to appear], all of which make use of our metric generality by being formulated over complete geodesic metric spaces of nonpositive curvature.

</details>


### [336] [FAB: A First-Order AB-based Gradient Algorithm for Distributed Bilevel Optimization over Time-Varying Directed Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06328)
*Yaoshuai Ma,Xiao Wang,Wei Yao,Jin Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对时变有向图上的分布式双层优化问题，提出了结合Push-Pull通信策略与基于值函数的惩罚方法的全一阶分布式梯度算法，解决了动态非平衡通信中的共识偏差和嵌套优化结构带来的挑战，并建立了非渐近收敛性。该分析框架的简化变体还解决了Push-Pull算法在时变有向图上收敛性的开放问题。实验验证了算法在超参数调优、数据超清洗和强化学习等任务中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式优化在时变有向图场景中面临超参数调优困难，经验观察表明双层优化可缓解该问题；同时，动态非平衡通信中的共识偏差与嵌套优化结构的复合挑战使得该问题尚待深入研究。

Method: 提出全一阶分布式梯度算法，将Push-Pull（AB）通信策略与基于值函数的惩罚方法相结合，用于求解时变有向图上的分布式双层优化问题。

Result: 建立了算法的非渐近收敛性；其分析框架的简化变体为Push-Pull算法在时变有向图上的收敛性提供了收敛率，解决了相关开放问题；在超参数调优、数据超清洗和强化学习任务中验证了算法效果。

Conclusion: 所提算法有效克服了时变有向图上分布式双层优化的复合挑战，且理论分析填补了Push-Pull算法在时变有向图上收敛性的理论空白。

Abstract: Distributed optimization over time-varying directed graphs has shown promising performance in addressing challenges posed by complex communication constraints in real-world scenarios. In many practical settings, however, the direct application of distributed optimization algorithms encounters additional difficulties, most notably hyperparameter tuning, which our empirical observations suggest can be effectively mitigated by integrating bilevel optimization. Motivated by these findings, we study distributed bilevel optimization over time-varying directed networks, a problem that remains largely unexplored due to the compounded challenges arising from consensus bias in dynamic unbalanced communication and the nested optimization structure. In this work, we propose a fully first-order distributed gradient-based algorithm that integrates the Push-Pull (also known as AB) communication strategy with a value function-based penalty method and establish its non-asymptotic convergence properties. Notably, a simplified variant of our analysis framework for nonconvex single-level distributed optimization establishes a convergence rate for the Push-Pull algorithm, thereby resolving an open question concerning its convergence over time-varying directed graphs. Empirical evaluations across diverse tasks, including hyperparameter tuning, data hyper-cleaning, and reinforcement learning, validate the effectiveness and efficiency of the proposed algorithm.

</details>


### [337] [Unbalanced Optimal Transport and Density Control for Discrete-Time Linear Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06391)
*Haruto Nakashima,Siddhartha Ganguly,Kenji Kashima*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了约束离散时间线性系统的不平衡最优传输（UOT）及其动态扩展——不平衡密度控制（UDC），证明了在高斯参考和线性系统下，这两个问题均可转化为全局最优的凸优化形式，并通过数值实验进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将不平衡最优传输（UOT）扩展到具有系统动力学和约束的离散时间线性系统中，解决传统最优传输无法处理不等总质量测度比较的问题，并建立与协方差控制（covariance steering）之间的联系。

Method: 针对高斯参考分布和离散时间线性系统，将UOT和UDC问题重新表述为凸优化问题，类似于协方差控制框架，从而获得全局最优解。

Result: 证明了UOT和UDC问题均存在全局最优的凸优化形式，并通过数值实验验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 不平衡最优传输及其动态扩展在不平衡密度控制问题中可被高效求解，为约束线性系统下的质量传输与密度调控提供了理论基础和可行算法。

Abstract: This article studies unbalanced optimal transport (UOT) and its dynamical extension, unbalanced density control (UDC), for a class of constrained discrete-time linear systems. UOT compares measures with unequal total mass by balancing transport cost and fidelity to reference measures, while UDC incorporates system dynamics and constraints into this framework. Focusing on Gaussian references and discrete-time linear systems, we show that both problems admit globally optimal convex formulations, analogous to covariance steering. A numerical experiment is provided to illustrate our approach.

</details>


### [338] [Second-Order Bilevel Optimization with Accelerated Convergence Rates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06431)
*Sheng Yang,Chengchang Liu,Lesi Chen,John C. S. Lui*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对非凸-强凸双层优化问题，提出了全二阶双层近似方法（FSBA）及其惰性变体（LFSBA），分别实现了$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(ε^{-1.5})$的迭代复杂度和更好的计算复杂度，并推广至极小极大优化中提出了LMCN方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究二阶方法在非凸-强凸双层优化中的应用，并解决二阶方法计算开销大的问题，探索能否比一阶方法实现加速收敛。

Method: 提出了全二阶双层近似方法（FSBA），并设计了惰性变体（LFSBA）通过跨迭代复用二阶信息来降低计算成本；还将类似思想应用于非凸强凹极小极大优化，提出惰性极小极大三次正则化牛顿方法（LMCN）。

Result: FSBA达到$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(ε^{-1.5})$迭代复杂度以找到超目标函数的$(ε, \mathcal{O}(\sqrtε))$二阶稳定点；LFSBA相比FSBA在计算复杂度上提升$\sqrt{d}$倍；LMCN相比现有二阶方法具有更好的计算复杂度。

Conclusion: 二阶方法在双层优化中能够比一阶方法实现加速收敛，通过惰性策略可有效降低二阶方法的计算负担。

Abstract: This paper studies second-order methods for nonconvex-strongly-convex bilevel optimization. We propose a novel fully second-order bilevel approximation method (FSBA) that achieves an iteration complexity of $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(ε^{-1.5})$ for finding the $(ε, \mathcal{O}(\sqrtε))$ second-order stationary point of the hyper-objective function. Our results demonstrate that second-order methods can achieve an accelerated convergence rate than first-order methods in bilevel optimization. To address the heavy computational cost associated with the second-order oracle, we introduce a lazy variant of FSBA, called LFSBA, which reuses second-order information across several iterations. We prove that LFSBA exhibits better computational complexity than FSBA by a factor of $\sqrt{d}$, where $d$ is the dimension of the problem. We also apply a similar idea to nonconvex strongly-concave minimax optimization and propose the lazy minimax cubic-regularized Newton (LMCN) method with better computational complexity compared to existing second-order methods.

</details>


### [339] [Performance guaranteed MPC Policy Approximation via Cost Guided Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06448)
*Chenchen Zhou,Yi Cao,Shuang-hua Yang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种代价引导的MPC近似方法，通过利用MPC问题的代价敏感性信息直接优化闭环性能损失，相比传统误差引导方法提供了更优的最优性损失保证，并在CSTR基准上显著提升了闭环性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统误差引导的学习方法通过最小化近似器输出与MPC最优控制动作之间的拟合误差来训练，但控制律设计的目标是最小化运行代价而非拟合误差，二者之间存在不一致性。

Method: 提出代价引导学习（Cost-Guided Learning）方法，利用MPC问题的代价敏感性信息（即代价函数相对于控制动作的梯度信息），直接最小化闭环性能损失，而非传统的最小化拟合误差。

Result: 理论分析证明代价引导学习相比误差引导学习能提供更紧的最优性损失保证；在连续搅拌釜反应器（CSTR）基准实验上，该方法得到的近似MPC策略实现了显著更好的闭环性能。

Conclusion: 本文通过将拟合误差与运行目标联系起来，克服了现有近似方法的关键局限性，其核心思想可更广泛地应用于数据驱动控制领域。

Abstract: Model predictive control (MPC) is widely used in industries but implementing it poses challenges due to hardware or time constraints. A promising solution is to approximate the MPC policy using function approximators like neural networks. Existing methods focus on minimizing the error between the approximators outputs and the MPC optimal control actions on training data, which is called error guided learning approach in this paper. However, the goals of control law design is not to minimize the fitting error but to minimize the operation cost. This paper proposes a novel cost-guided learning approach that utilizes the cost sensitivity information from the MPC problem to directly minimize the loss in closed-loop performance. A theoretical analysis shows cost-guided learning provides tighter guarantees on optimality loss compared to traditional error-guided learning. Experiments on a continuous stirred tank reactor (CSTR) benchmark demonstrate that the proposed technique results in approximate MPC policies that achieve substantially better closed-loop performance. This work makes an important contribution by connecting the fitting errors with operational objectives, overcoming key limitations of existing approximation methods. The core idea could be applied more broadly for data-driven control.

</details>


### [340] [Confidentiality of Linear Control Systems with Quadratic Output Under Sensor Attacks [Extended Version]](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06493)
*Zeyad M. Manaa,Nathan van de Wouw,Michelle S. Chong*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究线性控制系统在平衡点局部不可观时的状态估计问题，证明攻击者可通过注入特定信号使系统变得可观，并构建了实现局部指数收敛的观测器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决线性控制系统在平衡点局部不可观时，传统状态估计方法失效的问题，探索通过注入信号恢复可观性的可能性。

Method: 理论分析了注入信号诱导可观性的充要条件，在成功情形下设计了观测器，并通过仿真验证。

Result: 明确了注入信号能否诱导可观性的条件，并构造了使状态估计局部指数收敛到真实状态的观测器。

Conclusion: 即使系统在平衡点局部不可观，具有传感器读写能力的攻击者仍可通过注入信号诱导可观性，实现有效状态估计。

Abstract: We study the state estimation problem for linear control systems with quadratic outputs which are locally unobservable at the equilibrium. We show that, despite this inherent lack of observability, an adversary with sensor read and write capability can induce observability by injecting an appropriate signal into the measurement channel. Taking the role of an adversary, we characterize when an injected signal can or cannot induce observability and, in the successful case, construct an observer that achieves local exponential convergence of state estimates to the true states of the system. A simulation study demonstrates our results.

</details>


### [341] [Global self-optimizing control of batch processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06495)
*Chenchen Zhou,Hongxin Su,Xinhui Tang,Yi Cao,Shuang-hua Yang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对间歇过程的近最优操作问题，提出了扩展的全局自优化控制（gSOC）方法。通过向量化重新表述gSOC问题，解决了因果性带来的结构约束和非凸性挑战，并提出了一种快捷方法高效寻找次优解。在流加反应器案例中验证了方法的有效性，实现了简单且鲁棒的自优化控制方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 间歇过程具有强非线性和动态特性，现有全局自优化控制（gSOC）无法直接应用于此类过程，因为变量间的因果性导致了结构约束，使得被控变量（CV）选择问题更加困难。需要扩展gSOC以解决这些挑战。

Method: 将gSOC问题重新表述为向量化形式，证明结构约束在该形式下是线性的；提出一种快捷方法以高效寻找次优但更透明的解。

Result: 在流加反应器案例中，通过具有重复结构的组合矩阵构造被控变量，实现了简单的自优化控制方案，验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法简化了自优化控制的实施，增强了其实用适用性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: This work considers to achieve near-optimal operation for a class of batch processes by employing self-optimizing control (SOC). Comparing with a continuous one, a batch process exhibits stronger nonlinearity with dynamics because of the non-steady operation condition. This necessitates a global version of SOC to achieve satisfactory performance. Meanwhile, it also makes the existing global SOC (gSOC) not directly applicable to batch processes due to the causality amongst variables. Therefore, it is necessary to extend the original gSOC to batch processes. In addition to the nonconvexity challenge of the original gSOC problem, the new extension for batch processes has to face even more challenges. Particularly, the causality due to dynamics of batch processes brings in structural constraints on controlled variables (CVs), making a CV selection problem even more difficult. To address these challenges, the gSOC problem is recast in a vectorized formulation and it is proved that the structural constraints considered are linear in the vectorized formulation. Moreover, a novel shortcut method is proposed to efficiently find sub-optimal but more transparent solutions for this problem. The effectiveness of the new approach is validated through a case study of a fed-batch reactor, where CVs are constructed through a combination matrix with a repetitive structure, resulting in a simple SOC scheme. This simplicity facilitates the implementation of the SOC approach and enhances its practical applicability and robustness.

</details>


### [342] [MTRBO: Multiple trust-region based Bayesian optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06618)
*Sourav Das,Debjani Chakraborty,Pabitra Mitra*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多信任区域的贝叶斯优化方法（MTRBO），通过同时利用一个信任区域进行局部开发（利用后验均值）和另一个信任区域进行全局探索（利用后验方差），解决了高维贝叶斯优化中搜索空间指数增长、目标函数异质性和采样预算有限的问题，并在理论上证明了全局收敛性，在多种非凸高维测试函数和投资组合优化问题上取得了优于现有方法的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准贝叶斯优化在高维问题中效率低下，主要源于：搜索空间随维度指数增长、目标函数存在异质性（不同区域特性差异大）、以及采样预算有限。

Method: MTRBO方法维护多个信任区域：一个信任区域基于后验均值函数在当前最优解附近进行局部开发（exploitation）；另一个信任区域基于后验方差函数在搜索空间中最不确定的区域进行全局探索（exploration）。方法以高斯过程作为目标函数的先验假设，利用后验信息动态调整信任区域的位置和大小。

Result: 在多种非凸高维测试函数上，MTRBO相对于其他基于信任区域的贝叶斯优化算法展现出更优的解质量和更高效的采样预算利用率。在投资组合优化这一实际问题上验证了方法的实用性。

Conclusion: 多信任区域策略能够有效平衡高维贝叶斯优化中的探索与开发，理论上的全局收敛性保证了方法的可靠性，实验结果表明该方法在高维和实际场景中具有显著优势。

Abstract: Bayesian Optimization (BO) is a popular framework for optimizing black-box functions. Despite its effectiveness, BO is often inefficient for high-dimensional problems due to the exponential growth of the search space, heterogeneity of the objective function, and low sampling budget. To overcome these issues, this work proposes a multiple trust region-based Bayesian optimization technique(MTRBO). A trust region is a localized region within which an optimization model is trusted to approximate the objective function accurately. Assuming a Gaussian process (GP) as a prior belief about the objective function and based on the posterior mean and variance functions, the method adaptively exploits near the promising current solution inside a trust region. Also explores the most uncertain region in the search space inside another trust region. The theoretical global convergence property of the proposed method is established. Then the work is benchmarked against other state-of-the-art trust-region-based Bayesian optimization algorithms, demonstrating superior performance on a variety of non-convex and high-dimensional test functions. The proposed method outperforms others in terms of solution quality within the sampling budget (the number of function evaluations). The proposed method is applied to the portfolio optimization problem to verify its applicability in real-world scenarios.

</details>
